## CONTEXT

A refactoring block was applied to a Java project, but the configured Maven build failed.

The goal of this step is to repair the current workspace so that the applied refactoring block compiles and passes the configured build command.

This is a repair step, not a new refactoring step.

## TASK

Fix only the errors reported in the build log.

Use the current code state, the staged block, the files already changed by the executor, and the files mentioned by the build log to produce a minimal repair.

Do not redo the refactoring from scratch.

Do not change the refactoring strategy.

Do not undo the refactoring unless that is the smallest safe repair.

## CONSTRAINTS

- Modify only files listed in allowed_files.
- Return the full new content for every modified file.
- Do not return partial patches.
- Do not edit unrelated files.
- Do not edit unrelated tests.
- Do not remove tests.
- Do not weaken assertions.
- Do not change expected behavior to match a broken implementation.
- Do not introduce compatibility wrappers unless they are necessary to make the current refactoring compile.
- Do not undo previous successful blocks.
- If the error cannot be safely repaired using only allowed_files, return an empty files_to_write list and explain why in notes.
- Return only files that actually changed.
- Do not include unchanged files in files_to_write.

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.configuration;\n\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonProperty;\nimport com.github.benmanes.caffeine.cache.CaffeineSpec;\nimport io.dropwizard.jackson.Jackson;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Maps;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Resources;\nimport io.dropwizard.validation.BaseValidator;\nimport org.assertj.core.api.ThrowableAssertAlternative;\nimport org.assertj.core.data.MapEntry;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.validation.Valid;\nimport javax.validation.Validator;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Pattern;\nimport java.io.File;\nimport java.net.URISyntaxException;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.Enumeration;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Locale;\nimport java.util.Map;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatIllegalArgumentException;\n\npublic abstract class BaseConfigurationFactoryTest {\n\n    private static final String NEWLINE = System.lineSeparator();\n\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"UnusedDeclaration\")\n    public static class ExampleServer {\n\n        @JsonProperty\n        private int port = 8000;\n\n        public int getPort() {\n            return port;\n        }\n\n        public static ExampleServer create(int port) {\n            ExampleServer server = new ExampleServer();\n            server.port = port;\n            return server;\n        }\n\n    }\n\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"UnusedDeclaration\")\n    public static class Example {\n\n        @NotNull\n        @Pattern(regexp = \"[\\\\w]+[\\\\s]+[\\\\w]+([\\\\s][\\\\w]+)?\")\n        private String name = \"\";\n\n        @JsonProperty\n        private int age = 1;\n\n        List<String> type = Collections.emptyList();\n\n        @JsonProperty\n        private Map<String, String> properties = Collections.emptyMap();\n\n        @JsonProperty\n        private List<ExampleServer> servers = Collections.emptyList();\n\n        private boolean admin;\n\n        @JsonProperty(\"my.logger\")\n        private Map<String, String> logger = Collections.emptyMap();\n\n        public String getName() {\n            return name;\n        }\n\n        public List<String> getType() {\n            return type;\n        }\n\n        public Map<String, String> getProperties() {\n            return properties;\n        }\n\n        public List<ExampleServer> getServers() {\n            return servers;\n        }\n\n        public boolean isAdmin() {\n            return admin;\n        }\n\n        public void setAdmin(boolean admin) {\n            this.admin = admin;\n        }\n\n        public Map<String, String> getLogger() {\n            return logger;\n        }\n    }\n\n    static class ExampleWithDefaults {\n\n        @NotNull\n        @Pattern(regexp = \"[\\\\w]+[\\\\s]+[\\\\w]+([\\\\s][\\\\w]+)?\")\n        @JsonProperty\n        String name = \"Coda Hale\";\n\n        @JsonProperty\n        List<String> type = Arrays.asList(\"coder\", \"wizard\");\n\n        @JsonProperty\n        Map<String, String> properties = Maps.of(\"debug\", \"true\", \"settings.enabled\", \"false\");\n\n        @JsonProperty\n        List<ExampleServer> servers = Arrays.asList(\n                ExampleServer.create(8080), ExampleServer.create(8081), ExampleServer.create(8082));\n\n        @JsonProperty\n        @Valid\n        CaffeineSpec cacheBuilderSpec = CaffeineSpec.parse(\"initialCapacity=0,maximumSize=0\");\n    }\n\n    static class NonInsatiableExample {\n\n        @JsonProperty\n        String name = \"Code Hale\";\n\n        NonInsatiableExample(@JsonProperty(\"name\") String name) {\n            this.name = name;\n        }\n    }\n\n    protected final Validator validator = BaseValidator.newValidator();\n    protected ConfigurationFactory<Example> factory = new ConfigurationFactory<Example>() {\n        @Override\n        public Example build(ConfigurationSourceProvider provider, String path) {\n            return new Example();\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public Example build() {\n            return new Example();\n        }\n    };\n    protected File malformedFile = new File(\"/\");\n    protected File emptyFile = new File(\"/\");\n    protected File invalidFile = new File(\"/\");\n    protected File validFile = new File(\"/\");\n    protected File validNoTypeFile = new File(\"/\");\n    protected File typoFile = new File(\"/\");\n    protected File wrongTypeFile = new File(\"/\");\n    protected File malformedAdvancedFile = new File(\"/\");\n\n    protected static File resourceFileName(String resourceName) throws URISyntaxException {\n        return new File(Resources.getResource(resourceName).toURI());\n    }\n\n    @AfterEach\n    void resetConfigOverrides() {\n        for (Enumeration<?> props = System.getProperties().propertyNames(); props.hasMoreElements();) {\n            String keyString = (String) props.nextElement();\n            if (keyString.startsWith(\"dw.\")) {\n                System.clearProperty(keyString);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void usesDefaultedCacheBuilderSpec() throws Exception {\n        final ExampleWithDefaults example =\n            new YamlConfigurationFactory<>(ExampleWithDefaults.class, validator, Jackson.newObjectMapper(), \"dw\")\n                .build();\n        assertThat(example.cacheBuilderSpec)\n            .isNotNull()\n            .isEqualTo(CaffeineSpec.parse(\"initialCapacity=0,maximumSize=0\"));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void loadsValidConfigFiles() throws Exception {\n        final Example example = factory.build(validFile);\n\n        assertThat(example.getName())\n                .isEqualTo(\"Coda Hale\");\n\n        assertThat(example.getType().get(0))\n                .isEqualTo(\"coder\");\n        assertThat(example.getType().get(1))\n                .isEqualTo(\"wizard\");\n\n        assertThat(example.getProperties())\n                .contains(MapEntry.entry(\"debug\", \"true\"),\n                        MapEntry.entry(\"settings.enabled\", \"false\"));\n\n        assertThat(example.getServers())\n                .hasSize(3);\n        assertThat(example.getServers().get(0).getPort())\n                .isEqualTo(8080);\n\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void handlesSimpleOverride() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.name\", \"Coda Hale Overridden\");\n        final Example example = factory.build(validFile);\n        assertThat(example.getName())\n            .isEqualTo(\"Coda Hale Overridden\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void handlesExistingOverrideWithPeriod() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.my\\\\.logger.level\", \"debug\");\n        final Example example = factory.build(validFile);\n        assertThat(example.getLogger())\n            .containsEntry(\"level\", \"debug\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void handlesNewOverrideWithPeriod() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.my\\\\.logger.com\\\\.example\", \"error\");\n        final Example example = factory.build(validFile);\n        assertThat(example.getLogger())\n            .containsEntry(\"com.example\", \"error\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void handlesArrayOverride() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.type\", \"coder,wizard,overridden\");\n        final Example example = factory.build(validFile);\n        assertThat(example.getType().get(2))\n                .isEqualTo(\"overridden\");\n        assertThat(example.getType().size())\n                .isEqualTo(3);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void handlesArrayOverrideEscaped() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.type\", \"coder,wizard,overr\\\\,idden\");\n        final Example example = factory.build(validFile);\n        assertThat(example.getType().get(2))\n                .isEqualTo(\"overr,idden\");\n        assertThat(example.getType().size())\n                .isEqualTo(3);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void handlesSingleElementArrayOverride() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.type\", \"overridden\");\n        final Example example = factory.build(validFile);\n        assertThat(example.getType().get(0))\n                .isEqualTo(\"overridden\");\n        assertThat(example.getType().size())\n                .isEqualTo(1);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void handlesArrayOverrideIntoValidNoTypeFile() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.type\", \"coder,wizard,overridden\");\n        final Example example = factory.build(validNoTypeFile);\n        assertThat(example.getType().get(2))\n            .isEqualTo(\"overridden\");\n        assertThat(example.getType().size())\n            .isEqualTo(3);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void overridesArrayWithIndices() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.type[1]\", \"overridden\");\n        final Example example = factory.build(validFile);\n\n        assertThat(example.getType().get(0))\n                .isEqualTo(\"coder\");\n        assertThat(example.getType().get(1))\n                .isEqualTo(\"overridden\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void overridesArrayWithIndicesReverse() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.type[0]\", \"overridden\");\n        final Example example = factory.build(validFile);\n\n        assertThat(example.getType().get(0))\n                .isEqualTo(\"overridden\");\n        assertThat(example.getType().get(1))\n                .isEqualTo(\"wizard\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void overridesArrayPropertiesWithIndices() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.servers[0].port\", \"7000\");\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.servers[2].port\", \"9000\");\n        final Example example = factory.build(validFile);\n\n        assertThat(example.getServers())\n                .hasSize(3);\n        assertThat(example.getServers().get(0).getPort())\n                .isEqualTo(7000);\n        assertThat(example.getServers().get(2).getPort())\n                .isEqualTo(9000);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void overrideMapProperty() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.properties.settings.enabled\", \"true\");\n        final Example example = factory.build(validFile);\n        assertThat(example.getProperties())\n                .contains(MapEntry.entry(\"debug\", \"true\"),\n                        MapEntry.entry(\"settings.enabled\", \"true\"));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void throwsAnExceptionOnUnexpectedArrayOverride() {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.servers.port\", \"9000\");\n        assertThatIllegalArgumentException()\n            .isThrownBy(() -> factory.build(validFile))\n            .withMessageContaining(\"target is an array but no index specified\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void throwsAnExceptionOnArrayOverrideWithInvalidType() {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.servers\", \"one,two\");\n\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(ConfigurationParsingException.class).isThrownBy(() -> factory.build(validFile));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void throwsAnExceptionOnOverrideArrayIndexOutOfBounds() {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.type[2]\", \"invalid\");\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> factory.build(validFile))\n            .withMessageContaining(\"index is greater than size of array\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void throwsAnExceptionOnOverrideArrayPropertyIndexOutOfBounds() {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.servers[4].port\", \"9000\");\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> factory.build(validFile))\n            .withMessageContaining(\"index is greater than size of array\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void throwsAnExceptionOnMalformedFiles() {\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(ConfigurationParsingException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> factory.build(malformedFile));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void throwsAnExceptionOnEmptyFiles() {\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(ConfigurationParsingException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> factory.build(emptyFile))\n            .withMessageContaining(\" * Configuration at \" + emptyFile.toString() + \" must not be empty\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void throwsAnExceptionOnInvalidFiles() {\n        ThrowableAssertAlternative<ConfigurationValidationException> t = assertThatExceptionOfType(ConfigurationValidationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> factory.build(invalidFile));\n\n        if (\"en\".equals(Locale.getDefault().getLanguage())) {\n            t.withMessageEndingWith(String.format(\n                    \"%s has an error:%n  * name must match \\\"[\\\\w]+[\\\\s]+[\\\\w]+([\\\\s][\\\\w]+)?\\\"%n\",\n                    invalidFile.getName()));\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void handleOverrideDefaultConfiguration() throws Exception {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.name\", \"Coda Hale Overridden\");\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.type\", \"coder,wizard,overridden\");\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.properties.settings.enabled\", \"true\");\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.servers[0].port\", \"8090\");\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.servers[2].port\", \"8092\");\n\n        final ExampleWithDefaults example =\n                new YamlConfigurationFactory<>(ExampleWithDefaults.class, validator, Jackson.newObjectMapper(), \"dw\")\n                        .build();\n\n        assertThat(example.name).isEqualTo(\"Coda Hale Overridden\");\n        assertThat(example.type.get(2)).isEqualTo(\"overridden\");\n        assertThat(example.type.size()).isEqualTo(3);\n        assertThat(example.properties).containsEntry(\"settings.enabled\", \"true\");\n        assertThat(example.servers.get(0).getPort()).isEqualTo(8090);\n        assertThat(example.servers.get(2).getPort()).isEqualTo(8092);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void handleDefaultConfigurationWithoutOverriding() throws Exception {\n        final ExampleWithDefaults example =\n                new YamlConfigurationFactory<>(ExampleWithDefaults.class, validator, Jackson.newObjectMapper(), \"dw\")\n                        .build();\n\n        assertThat(example.name).isEqualTo(\"Coda Hale\");\n        assertThat(example.type).isEqualTo(Arrays.asList(\"coder\", \"wizard\"));\n        assertThat(example.properties).isEqualTo(Maps.of(\"debug\", \"true\", \"settings.enabled\", \"false\"));\n        assertThat(example.servers.get(0).getPort()).isEqualTo(8080);\n        assertThat(example.servers.get(1).getPort()).isEqualTo(8081);\n        assertThat(example.servers.get(2).getPort()).isEqualTo(8082);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void throwsAnExceptionIfDefaultConfigurationCantBeInstantiated() {\n        System.setProperty(\"dw.name\", \"Coda Hale Overridden\");\n        final YamlConfigurationFactory<NonInsatiableExample> factory =\n            new YamlConfigurationFactory<>(NonInsatiableExample.class, validator, Jackson.newObjectMapper(), \"dw\");\n        assertThatIllegalArgumentException()\n            .isThrownBy(factory::build)\n            .withMessage(\"Unable to create an instance of the configuration class: \" +\n                \"'io.dropwizard.configuration.BaseConfigurationFactoryTest.NonInsatiableExample'\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void incorrectTypeIsFound() {\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(ConfigurationParsingException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> factory.build(wrongTypeFile))\n            .withMessage(String.format(\"%s has an error:\" + NEWLINE +\n                \"  * Incorrect type of value at: age; is of type: String, expected: int\" + NEWLINE, wrongTypeFile));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void printsDetailedInformationOnMalformedContent() throws Exception {\n        factory.build(malformedAdvancedFile);\n    }\n}\n"

      "path": "dropwizard-jackson/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/jackson/CaffeineModule.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jackson;\n\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonGenerator;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonParser;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.core.Version;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.BeanDescription;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.DeserializationConfig;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.DeserializationContext;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JavaType;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonDeserializer;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonMappingException;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonSerializer;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.Module;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.SerializationConfig;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.SerializerProvider;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.deser.Deserializers;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ser.Serializers;\nimport com.github.benmanes.caffeine.cache.CaffeineSpec;\n\nimport java.io.IOException;\n\n/**\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic class CaffeineModule extends Module {\n    private static class CaffeineSpecDeserializer extends JsonDeserializer<CaffeineSpec> {\n        @Override\n        public CaffeineSpec deserialize(JsonParser jp,\n                                        DeserializationContext ctxt) throws IOException {\n            final String text = jp.getText();\n            final boolean disabled = \"off\".equalsIgnoreCase(text) || \"disabled\".equalsIgnoreCase(text);\n            return CaffeineSpec.parse(disabled ? \"initialCapacity=0,maximumSize=0\" : text);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static class CaffeineSpecSerializer extends JsonSerializer<CaffeineSpec> {\n        @Override\n        public void serialize(CaffeineSpec value, JsonGenerator gen, SerializerProvider serializers) throws IOException {\n            gen.writeString(value.toParsableString());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static class CaffeineDeserializers extends Deserializers.Base {\n        @Override\n        public JsonDeserializer<?> findBeanDeserializer(JavaType type,\n                                                        DeserializationConfig config,\n                                                        BeanDescription beanDesc) throws JsonMappingException {\n            if (CaffeineSpec.class.isAssignableFrom(type.getRawClass())) {\n                return new CaffeineSpecDeserializer();\n            }\n\n            return super.findBeanDeserializer(type, config, beanDesc);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static class CaffeineSerializers extends Serializers.Base {\n        @Override\n        public JsonSerializer<?> findSerializer(SerializationConfig config, JavaType type, BeanDescription beanDesc) {\n            if (CaffeineSpec.class.isAssignableFrom(type.getRawClass())) {\n                return new CaffeineSpecSerializer();\n            }\n\n            return super.findSerializer(config, type, beanDesc);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String getModuleName() {\n        return this.getClass().getSimpleName();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public Version version() {\n        return Version.unknownVersion();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void setupModule(SetupContext context) {\n        context.addDeserializers(new CaffeineDeserializers());\n        context.addSerializers(new CaffeineSerializers());\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-jackson/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/jackson/DiscoverableSubtypeResolver.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jackson;\n\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.jsontype.impl.StdSubtypeResolver;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Nullable;\nimport java.io.BufferedReader;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.InputStream;\nimport java.io.InputStreamReader;\nimport java.net.URL;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Enumeration;\nimport java.util.List;\n\n/**\n * A subtype resolver which discovers subtypes via\n * {@code META-INF/services/io.dropwizard.jackson.Discoverable}.\n */\npublic class DiscoverableSubtypeResolver extends StdSubtypeResolver {\n    private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;\n    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(DiscoverableSubtypeResolver.class);\n\n    private final List<Class<?>> discoveredSubtypes;\n\n    public DiscoverableSubtypeResolver() {\n        this(Discoverable.class);\n    }\n\n    public DiscoverableSubtypeResolver(Class<?> rootKlass) {\n        final List<Class<?>> subtypes = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Class<?> klass : discoverServices(rootKlass)) {\n            for (Class<?> subtype : discoverServices(klass)) {\n                subtypes.add(subtype);\n                registerSubtypes(subtype);\n            }\n        }\n        this.discoveredSubtypes = subtypes;\n    }\n\n    public List<Class<?>> getDiscoveredSubtypes() {\n        return discoveredSubtypes;\n    }\n\n    protected ClassLoader getClassLoader() {\n        return this.getClass().getClassLoader();\n    }\n\n    protected List<Class<?>> discoverServices(Class<?> klass) {\n        final List<Class<?>> serviceClasses = new ArrayList<>();\n        try {\n            // use classloader that loaded this class to find the service descriptors on the classpath\n            // better than ClassLoader.getSystemResources() which may not be the same classloader if ths app\n            // is running in a container (e.g. via maven exec:java)\n            final Enumeration<URL> resources = getClassLoader().getResources(\"META-INF/services/\" + klass.getName());\n            while (resources.hasMoreElements()) {\n                final URL url = resources.nextElement();\n                try (InputStream input = url.openStream();\n                     InputStreamReader streamReader = new InputStreamReader(input, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n                     BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(streamReader)) {\n                    String line;\n                    while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {\n                        if (!line.startsWith(\"#\")) {\n                            final Class<?> loadedClass = loadClass(line);\n                            if (loadedClass != null) {\n                                serviceClasses.add(loadedClass);\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        } catch (IOException e) {\n            LOGGER.warn(\"Unable to load META-INF/services/{}\", klass.getName(), e);\n        }\n        return serviceClasses;\n    }\n\n    @Nullable\n    private Class<?> loadClass(String line) {\n        try {\n            return getClassLoader().loadClass(line.trim());\n        } catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {\n            LOGGER.info(\"Unable to load {}\", line);\n            return null;\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/DropwizardResourceConfig.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey;\n\nimport com.codahale.metrics.Clock;\nimport com.codahale.metrics.MetricRegistry;\nimport com.codahale.metrics.jersey2.InstrumentedResourceMethodApplicationListener;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.ResolvedType;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.TypeResolver;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.caching.CacheControlledResponseFeature;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.params.AbstractParamConverterProvider;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.sessions.SessionFactoryProvider;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.validation.FuzzyEnumParamConverterProvider;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.JavaVersion;\nimport javassist.ClassPool;\nimport javassist.CtClass;\nimport javassist.LoaderClassPath;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.internal.inject.AbstractBinder;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.internal.inject.Providers;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.ServerProperties;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.model.Resource;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.model.ResourceMethod;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.monitoring.ApplicationEvent;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.monitoring.ApplicationEventListener;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.monitoring.RequestEvent;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.monitoring.RequestEventListener;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Nullable;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;\nimport java.io.Serializable;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.Comparator;\nimport java.util.HashSet;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Objects;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport java.util.TreeSet;\nimport java.util.UUID;\nimport java.util.regex.Pattern;\nimport java.util.stream.Collectors;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\npublic class DropwizardResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {\n    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(DropwizardResourceConfig.class);\n    private static final String NEWLINE = String.format(\"%n\");\n    private static final TypeResolver TYPE_RESOLVER = new TypeResolver();\n\n    private static final Pattern PATH_DIRTY_SLASHES = Pattern.compile(\"\\\\s*/\\\\s*/+\\\\s*\");\n\n    private String urlPattern = \"/*\";\n    private String contextPath = \"/\";\n    private final ComponentLoggingListener loggingListener = new ComponentLoggingListener(this);\n\n    public DropwizardResourceConfig() {\n        this(null);\n    }\n\n    public DropwizardResourceConfig(@Nullable MetricRegistry metricRegistry) {\n        super();\n\n        if (metricRegistry == null) {\n            metricRegistry = new MetricRegistry();\n        }\n\n        property(ServerProperties.WADL_FEATURE_DISABLE, Boolean.TRUE);\n        register(loggingListener);\n\n        register(new MetricRegistryBinder(metricRegistry));\n        register(new InstrumentedResourceMethodApplicationListener(metricRegistry, Clock.defaultClock(), true));\n        register(CacheControlledResponseFeature.class);\n        register(io.dropwizard.jersey.guava.OptionalMessageBodyWriter.class);\n        register(new io.dropwizard.jersey.guava.OptionalParamBinder());\n        register(io.dropwizard.jersey.optional.OptionalMessageBodyWriter.class);\n        register(io.dropwizard.jersey.optional.OptionalDoubleMessageBodyWriter.class);\n        register(io.dropwizard.jersey.optional.OptionalIntMessageBodyWriter.class);\n        register(io.dropwizard.jersey.optional.OptionalLongMessageBodyWriter.class);\n        register(new io.dropwizard.jersey.optional.OptionalParamBinder());\n        register(AbstractParamConverterProvider.class);\n        register(new FuzzyEnumParamConverterProvider());\n        register(new SessionFactoryProvider.Binder());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Build a {@link DropwizardResourceConfig} which makes Jersey Test run on a random port,\n     * also see {@code org.glassfish.jersey.test.TestProperties#CONTAINER_PORT}.\n     *\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    public static DropwizardResourceConfig forTesting() {\n        return forTesting(null);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Build a {@link DropwizardResourceConfig} which makes Jersey Test run on a random port,\n     * also see {@code org.glassfish.jersey.test.TestProperties#CONTAINER_PORT}.\n     *\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    public static DropwizardResourceConfig forTesting(@Nullable MetricRegistry metricRegistry) {\n        final DropwizardResourceConfig config = new DropwizardResourceConfig(metricRegistry);\n        // See org.glassfish.jersey.test.TestProperties#CONTAINER_PORT\n        config.property(\"jersey.config.test.container.port\", \"0\");\n        return config;\n    }\n\n    public String getUrlPattern() {\n        return urlPattern;\n    }\n\n    public void setUrlPattern(String urlPattern) {\n        this.urlPattern = urlPattern;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    public String getContextPath() {\n        return contextPath;\n    }\n\n    public void setContextPath(String contextPath) {\n        this.contextPath = contextPath;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    public String getEndpointsInfo() {\n        return loggingListener.getEndpointsInfo();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Combines types of getClasses() and getSingletons in one Set.\n     *\n     * @return all registered types\n     */\n    Set<Class<?>> allClasses() {\n        final Set<Class<?>> allClasses = new HashSet<>(getClasses());\n        for (Object singleton : getSingletons()) {\n            allClasses.add(singleton.getClass());\n        }\n        return allClasses;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public ResourceConfig register(final Object component) {\n        final Object object = requireNonNull(component);\n        Class<?> clazz = object.getClass();\n        // If a class gets passed through as an object, cast to Class and register directly\n        if (component instanceof Class<?>) {\n            return super.register((Class<?>) component);\n        } else if (Providers.isProvider(clazz) || org.glassfish.hk2.utilities.Binder.class.isAssignableFrom(clazz)) {\n            // If Jersey supports this component's class (including Binders), register directly\n            return super.register(object);\n        } else {\n            // Else register a binder that binds the instance to its class type\n            try {\n                // Need to create a new subclass dynamically here because Jersey\n                // doesn't add new bindings for the same class\n                final ClassPool pool = ClassPool.getDefault();\n                pool.insertClassPath(new LoaderClassPath(this.getClass().getClassLoader()));\n                final CtClass cc = pool.makeClass(SpecificBinder.class.getName() + UUID.randomUUID());\n                cc.setSuperclass(pool.get(SpecificBinder.class.getName()));\n                final Object binderProxy;\n                if (JavaVersion.isJava8()) {\n                    binderProxy = cc.toClass().getConstructor(Object.class, Class.class).newInstance(object, clazz);\n                } else {\n                    binderProxy = cc.toClass(SpecificBinder.class).getConstructor(Object.class, Class.class).newInstance(object, clazz);\n                }\n                super.register(binderProxy);\n                return super.register(clazz);\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                throw new RuntimeException(e);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    static String cleanUpPath(String path) {\n        return PATH_DIRTY_SLASHES.matcher(path).replaceAll(\"/\").trim();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    public static class SpecificBinder extends AbstractBinder {\n        private Object object;\n        private Class<?> clazz;\n\n        public SpecificBinder(Object object, Class<?> clazz) {\n            this.object = object;\n            this.clazz = clazz;\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public void configure() {\n            bind(object).to(clazz);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static class EndpointLogLine {\n        private final String httpMethod;\n        private final String basePath;\n        private final Class<?> klass;\n\n        EndpointLogLine(String httpMethod, String basePath, Class<?> klass) {\n            this.basePath = basePath;\n            this.klass = klass;\n            this.httpMethod = httpMethod;\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public String toString() {\n            final String method = httpMethod == null ? \"UNKNOWN\" : httpMethod;\n            return String.format(\"    %-7s %s (%s)\", method, basePath, klass.getCanonicalName());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static class EndpointComparator implements Comparator<EndpointLogLine>, Serializable {\n        private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;\n\n        @Override\n        public int compare(EndpointLogLine endpointA, EndpointLogLine endpointB) {\n            return Comparator.<EndpointLogLine, String>comparing(endpoint -> endpoint.basePath)\n                    .thenComparing(endpoint -> endpoint.httpMethod, Comparator.nullsLast(Comparator.naturalOrder()))\n                    .compare(endpointA, endpointB);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static class ComponentLoggingListener implements ApplicationEventListener {\n        private final DropwizardResourceConfig config;\n        private List<Resource> resources = Collections.emptyList();\n        private Set<Class<?>> providers = Collections.emptySet();\n\n        ComponentLoggingListener(DropwizardResourceConfig config) {\n            this.config = config;\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"Slf4jFormatShouldBeConst\")\n        public void onEvent(ApplicationEvent event) {\n            if (event.getType() == ApplicationEvent.Type.INITIALIZATION_APP_FINISHED) {\n                resources = event.getResourceModel().getResources();\n                providers = event.getProviders();\n\n                final String resourceClasses = resources.stream()\n                        .map(x -> x.getClass().getCanonicalName())\n                        .collect(Collectors.joining(\", \"));\n\n                final String providerClasses = providers.stream()\n                        .map(Class::getCanonicalName)\n                        .collect(Collectors.joining(\", \"));\n\n                LOGGER.debug(\"resources = {}\", resourceClasses);\n                LOGGER.debug(\"providers = {}\", providerClasses);\n                LOGGER.info(getEndpointsInfo());\n            }\n        }\n\n        private List<EndpointLogLine> logMethodLines(Resource resource, String contextPath) {\n            final List<EndpointLogLine> methodLines = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (ResourceMethod method : resource.getAllMethods()) {\n                if (\"OPTIONS\".equalsIgnoreCase(method.getHttpMethod())) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                final String path = mergePaths(contextPath, resource.getPath());\n                final Class<?> handler = method.getInvocable().getHandler().getHandlerClass();\n                switch (method.getType()) {\n                    case RESOURCE_METHOD:\n                        methodLines.add(new EndpointLogLine(method.getHttpMethod(), path, handler));\n                        break;\n                    case SUB_RESOURCE_LOCATOR:\n                        final ResolvedType responseType = TYPE_RESOLVER\n                                .resolve(method.getInvocable().getResponseType());\n                        final Class<?> erasedType = !responseType.getTypeBindings().isEmpty() ?\n                                responseType.getTypeBindings().getBoundType(0).getErasedType() :\n                                responseType.getErasedType();\n\n                        final Resource res = Resource.from(erasedType);\n                        if (res == null) {\n                            methodLines.add(new EndpointLogLine(method.getHttpMethod(), path, handler));\n                        } else {\n                            methodLines.addAll(logResourceLines(res, path));\n                        }\n\n                        break;\n                    default:\n                        break;\n                }\n            }\n\n            return methodLines;\n        }\n\n        private static String mergePaths(@NotNull String context, String subPath) {\n            if (subPath == null || subPath.isEmpty()) {\n                return cleanUpPath(context);\n            }\n\n            final StringBuilder path = new StringBuilder(context);\n            if (!context.endsWith(\"/\")) {\n                path.append('/');\n            }\n            if (!\"/\".equals(subPath)) {\n                final int startIndex = subPath.startsWith(\"/\") ? 1 : 0;\n                final int endIndex = subPath.endsWith(\"/\") ? subPath.length() - 1 : subPath.length();\n                path.append(subPath, startIndex, endIndex);\n            }\n\n            return cleanUpPath(path.toString());\n        }\n\n        private List<EndpointLogLine> logResourceLines(Resource resource, String contextPath) {\n            final List<EndpointLogLine> resourceLines = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (Resource child : resource.getChildResources()) {\n                resourceLines.addAll(logResourceLines(child, mergePaths(contextPath, resource.getPath())));\n            }\n\n            resourceLines.addAll(logMethodLines(resource, contextPath));\n\n            return resourceLines;\n        }\n\n        String getEndpointsInfo() {\n            final StringBuilder msg = new StringBuilder(1024);\n            final Set<EndpointLogLine> endpointLogLines = new TreeSet<>(new EndpointComparator());\n            final String contextPath = config.getContextPath();\n            final String normalizedContextPath = contextPath.isEmpty() || contextPath.equals(\"/\") ? \"\" :\n                    contextPath.startsWith(\"/\") ? contextPath : \"/\" + contextPath;\n            final String pattern = config.getUrlPattern().endsWith(\"/*\") ?\n                    config.getUrlPattern().substring(0, config.getUrlPattern().length() - 1) :\n                    config.getUrlPattern();\n\n            final String path = mergePaths(normalizedContextPath, pattern);\n\n            msg.append(\"The following paths were found for the configured resources:\");\n            msg.append(NEWLINE).append(NEWLINE);\n\n            for (Resource resource : resources) {\n                endpointLogLines.addAll(logResourceLines(resource, path));\n            }\n\n            final List<EndpointLogLine> providerLines = providers.stream()\n                    .map(Resource::from)\n                    .filter(Objects::nonNull)\n                    .flatMap(res -> logResourceLines(res, path).stream())\n                    .collect(Collectors.toList());\n\n            endpointLogLines.addAll(providerLines);\n\n            if (!endpointLogLines.isEmpty()) {\n                for (EndpointLogLine line : endpointLogLines) {\n                    msg.append(line).append(NEWLINE);\n                }\n            } else {\n                msg.append(\"    NONE\").append(NEWLINE);\n            }\n\n            return msg.toString();\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        @Nullable\n        public RequestEventListener onRequest(RequestEvent requestEvent) {\n            return null;\n        }\n    }\n\n    static final class MetricRegistryBinder extends AbstractBinder {\n        private final MetricRegistry metricRegistry;\n\n        public MetricRegistryBinder(MetricRegistry metricRegistry) {\n            this.metricRegistry = metricRegistry;\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        protected void configure() {\n                bind(metricRegistry).to(MetricRegistry.class);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.errors;\n\nimport org.eclipse.jetty.io.EofException;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Response;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.Provider;\n\n/**\n* This class is intended to catch Early EOF errors that occur when the client disconnects while the server is reading\n* from the input stream.\n*\n* We catch the org.ecplise.jetty.io.EofException rather than the more generic java.io.EOFException to ensure that we're\n* only catching jetty server based errors where the client disconnects, as specified by {@link EofException}.\n*/\n@Provider\npublic class EarlyEofExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<EofException> {\n\n    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(EarlyEofExceptionMapper.class);\n\n    @Override\n    public Response toResponse(EofException e) {\n        LOGGER.debug(\"EOF Exception encountered - client disconnected during stream processing.\", e);\n\n        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST).build();\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/errors/EofExceptionWriterInterceptor.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.errors;\n\nimport com.codahale.metrics.Counter;\nimport com.codahale.metrics.MetricRegistry;\nimport org.eclipse.jetty.io.EofException;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Priority;\nimport javax.inject.Inject;\nimport javax.ws.rs.WebApplicationException;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.Provider;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.WriterInterceptor;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.WriterInterceptorContext;\nimport java.io.IOException;\n\n/**\n * A {@link WriterInterceptor} to swallow {@link org.eclipse.jetty.io.EofException} which occurs when a client\n * disconnects before the complete response could be sent.\n *\n * @see EarlyEofExceptionMapper\n * @see EofException\n */\n@Provider\n@Priority(Integer.MAX_VALUE)\npublic class EofExceptionWriterInterceptor implements WriterInterceptor {\n    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(EofExceptionWriterInterceptor.class);\n\n    private final Counter exceptionCounter;\n\n    @Inject\n    public EofExceptionWriterInterceptor(MetricRegistry metricRegistry) {\n        this.exceptionCounter = metricRegistry.counter(MetricRegistry.name(getClass(), \"eof-exceptions\"));\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void aroundWriteTo(WriterInterceptorContext context) throws IOException, WebApplicationException {\n        try {\n            context.proceed();\n        } catch (EofException e) {\n            LOGGER.debug(\"Client disconnected while processing and sending response\", e);\n            exceptionCounter.inc();\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.errors;\n\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonCreator;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonProperty;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Nullable;\nimport java.util.Objects;\n\n@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)\npublic class ErrorMessage {\n    private final int code;\n    private final String message;\n\n    @Nullable\n    private final String details;\n\n    public ErrorMessage(String message) {\n        this(500, message);\n    }\n\n    public ErrorMessage(int code, String message) {\n        this(code, message, null);\n    }\n\n    @JsonCreator\n    public ErrorMessage(@JsonProperty(\"code\") int code, @JsonProperty(\"message\") String message,\n                        @Nullable @JsonProperty(\"details\") String details) {\n        this.code = code;\n        this.message = message;\n        this.details = details;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty(\"code\")\n    public Integer getCode() {\n        return code;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty(\"message\")\n    public String getMessage() {\n        return message;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty(\"details\")\n    @Nullable\n    public String getDetails() {\n        return details;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean equals(final Object obj) {\n        if (this == obj) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        if ((obj == null) || (getClass() != obj.getClass())) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        final ErrorMessage other = (ErrorMessage) obj;\n        return Objects.equals(code, other.code)\n                && Objects.equals(message, other.message)\n                && Objects.equals(details, other.details);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public int hashCode() {\n        return Objects.hash(code, message, details);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n        return \"ErrorMessage{code=\" + code + \", message='\" + message + \"', details='\" + details + \"'}\";\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/errors/LoggingExceptionMapper.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.errors;\n\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.WebApplicationException;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.MediaType;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Response;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.Provider;\nimport java.util.Locale;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\n@Provider\npublic abstract class LoggingExceptionMapper<E extends Throwable> implements ExceptionMapper<E> {\n    protected final Logger logger;\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    protected LoggingExceptionMapper(Logger logger) {\n        this.logger = requireNonNull(logger, \"logger\");\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    public LoggingExceptionMapper() {\n        this(LoggerFactory.getLogger(LoggingExceptionMapper.class));\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public Response toResponse(E exception) {\n        // If we're dealing with a web exception, we can service certain types of request (like\n        // redirection or server errors) better and also propagate properties of the inner response.\n        if (exception instanceof WebApplicationException) {\n            final Response response = ((WebApplicationException) exception).getResponse();\n            Response.Status.Family family = response.getStatusInfo().getFamily();\n            if (family.equals(Response.Status.Family.REDIRECTION)) {\n                return response;\n            }\n            if (family.equals(Response.Status.Family.SERVER_ERROR)) {\n                logException(exception);\n            }\n\n            return Response.fromResponse(response)\n                    .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)\n                    .entity(new ErrorMessage(response.getStatus(), exception.getLocalizedMessage()))\n                    .build();\n        }\n\n        // Else the thrown exception is a not a web exception, so the exception is most likely\n        // unexpected. We'll create a unique id in the server error response that is also logged for\n        // correlation\n        final long id = logException(exception);\n        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR.getStatusCode())\n                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)\n                .entity(new ErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(id, exception)))\n                .build();\n    }\n\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"UnusedParameters\")\n    protected String formatErrorMessage(long id, E exception) {\n        return String.format(Locale.ROOT, \"There was an error processing your request. It has been logged (ID %016x).\", id);\n    }\n\n    protected long logException(E exception) {\n        final long id = ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextLong();\n        logException(id, exception);\n        return id;\n    }\n\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"Slf4jFormatShouldBeConst\")\n    protected void logException(long id, E exception) {\n        logger.error(formatLogMessage(id, exception), exception);\n    }\n\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"UnusedParameters\")\n    protected String formatLogMessage(long id, Throwable exception) {\n        return String.format(Locale.ROOT, \"Error handling a request: %016x\", id);\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/filter/AllowedMethodsFilter.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.filter;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Sets;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.servlet.Filter;\nimport javax.servlet.FilterChain;\nimport javax.servlet.FilterConfig;\nimport javax.servlet.ServletException;\nimport javax.servlet.ServletRequest;\nimport javax.servlet.ServletResponse;\nimport javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;\nimport javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.Optional;\nimport java.util.Set;\n\npublic class AllowedMethodsFilter implements Filter {\n\n    public static final String ALLOWED_METHODS_PARAM = \"allowedMethods\";\n    public static final Set<String> DEFAULT_ALLOWED_METHODS = Sets.of(\n            \"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"DELETE\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\", \"PATCH\"\n    );\n\n    private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger(AllowedMethodsFilter.class);\n\n    private Set<String> allowedMethods = Collections.emptySet();\n\n    @Override\n    public void init(FilterConfig config) {\n        allowedMethods = Optional.ofNullable(config.getInitParameter(ALLOWED_METHODS_PARAM))\n            .map(p -> Sets.of(p.split(\",\")))\n            .orElse(DEFAULT_ALLOWED_METHODS);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void doFilter(ServletRequest request, ServletResponse response, FilterChain chain)\n            throws IOException, ServletException {\n        handle((HttpServletRequest) request, (HttpServletResponse) response, chain);\n    }\n\n    private void handle(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response, FilterChain chain)\n            throws IOException, ServletException {\n        if (allowedMethods.contains(request.getMethod())) {\n            chain.doFilter(request, response);\n        } else {\n            LOG.debug(\"Request with disallowed method {} blocked\", request.getMethod());\n            response.sendError(HttpServletResponse.SC_METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void destroy() {\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/filter/RequestIdFilter.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.filter;\n\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Priority;\nimport javax.ws.rs.Priorities;\nimport javax.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;\nimport javax.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseContext;\nimport javax.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseFilter;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.Provider;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.util.Optional;\nimport java.util.Random;\nimport java.util.UUID;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;\n\n/**\n * This class adds a \"X-Request-Id\" HTTP response header and logs the following\n * information: request method, request path, request ID, response status,\n * response length (or -1 if not known).\n *\n * @see <a href=\"https://devcenter.heroku.com/articles/http-request-id\">Heroku - HTTP Request IDs</a>\n */\n@Provider\n@Priority(Priorities.USER)\npublic class RequestIdFilter implements ContainerResponseFilter {\n\n    private static final String REQUEST_ID = \"X-Request-Id\";\n\n    private Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(RequestIdFilter.class);\n\n    void setLogger(Logger logger) {\n        this.logger = logger;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void filter(final ContainerRequestContext request,\n            final ContainerResponseContext response) throws IOException {\n\n        String id = Optional.ofNullable(request.getHeaderString(REQUEST_ID))\n            .filter(header -> !header.isEmpty())\n            .orElseGet(() -> generateRandomUuid().toString());\n\n        logger.trace(\"method={} path={} request_id={} status={} length={}\",\n                request.getMethod(), request.getUriInfo().getPath(), id,\n                response.getStatus(), response.getLength());\n        response.getHeaders().putSingle(REQUEST_ID, id);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Generate a random UUID v4 that will perform reasonably when used by\n     * multiple threads under load.\n     *\n     * @see <a href=\"https://github.com/Netflix/netflix-commons/blob/v0.3.0/netflix-commons-util/src/main/java/com/netflix/util/concurrent/ConcurrentUUIDFactory.java\">ConcurrentUUIDFactory</a>\n     * @return random UUID\n     */\n    private static UUID generateRandomUuid() {\n        final Random rnd = ThreadLocalRandom.current();\n        long mostSig  = rnd.nextLong();\n        long leastSig = rnd.nextLong();\n\n        // Identify this as a version 4 UUID, that is one based on a random value.\n        mostSig &= 0xffffffffffff0fffL;\n        mostSig |= 0x0000000000004000L;\n\n        // Set the variant identifier as specified for version 4 UUID values.  The two\n        // high order bits of the lower word are required to be one and zero, respectively.\n        leastSig &= 0x3fffffffffffffffL;\n        leastSig |= 0x8000000000000000L;\n\n        return new UUID(mostSig, leastSig);\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.optional;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.optional.exception.EmptyOptionalException;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.Produces;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.MediaType;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.MultivaluedMap;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.MessageBodyWriter;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.Provider;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.OutputStream;\nimport java.lang.annotation.Annotation;\nimport java.lang.reflect.Type;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.util.OptionalDouble;\n\n@Provider\n@Produces(MediaType.WILDCARD)\npublic class OptionalDoubleMessageBodyWriter implements MessageBodyWriter<OptionalDouble> {\n    // Jersey ignores this\n    @Override\n    public long getSize(OptionalDouble entity, Class<?> type, Type genericType, Annotation[] annotations, MediaType mediaType) {\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isWriteable(Class<?> type, Type genericType, Annotation[] annotations, MediaType mediaType) {\n        return OptionalDouble.class.isAssignableFrom(type);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void writeTo(OptionalDouble entity,\n                        Class<?> type,\n                        Type genericType,\n                        Annotation[] annotations,\n                        MediaType mediaType,\n                        MultivaluedMap<String, Object> httpHeaders,\n                        OutputStream entityStream) throws IOException {\n        if (!entity.isPresent()) {\n            throw EmptyOptionalException.INSTANCE;\n        }\n\n        entityStream.write(Double.toString(entity.getAsDouble()).getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII));\n    }\n}\n"
    },

javax.annotation.Nullable;\nimport javax.inject.Inject;\nimport javax.ws.rs.Produces;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.MediaType;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.MultivaluedMap;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.MessageBodyWriter;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.Provider;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.OutputStream;\nimport java.lang.annotation.Annotation;\nimport java.lang.reflect.ParameterizedType;\nimport java.lang.reflect.Type;\nimport java.util.Optional;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\n@Provider\n@Produces(MediaType.WILDCARD)\npublic class OptionalMessageBodyWriter implements MessageBodyWriter<Optional<?>> {\n\n    @Inject\n    @Nullable\n    private javax.inject.Provider<MessageBodyWorkers> mbw;\n\n    // Jersey ignores this\n    @Override\n    public long getSize(Optional<?> entity, Class<?> type, Type genericType,\n                        Annotation[] annotations, MediaType mediaType) {\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isWriteable(Class<?> type, Type genericType,\n                               Annotation[] annotations, MediaType mediaType) {\n        return Optional.class.isAssignableFrom(type);\n    }\n\n    @SuppressWarnings({\"rawtypes\", \"unchecked\"})\n    @Override\n    public void writeTo(Optional<?> entity,\n                        Class<?> type,\n                        Type genericType,\n                        Annotation[] annotations,\n                        MediaType mediaType,\n                        MultivaluedMap<String, Object> httpHeaders,\n                        OutputStream entityStream)\n            throws IOException {\n        if (!entity.isPresent()) {\n            throw EmptyOptionalException.INSTANCE;\n        }\n\n        final Type innerGenericType = (genericType instanceof ParameterizedType) ?\n            ((ParameterizedType) genericType).getActualTypeArguments()[0] : entity.get().getClass();\n\n        final MessageBodyWriter writer = requireNonNull(mbw).get().getMessageBodyWriter(entity.get().getClass(),\n            innerGenericType, annotations, mediaType);\n        writer.writeTo(entity.get(), entity.get().getClass(),\n            innerGenericType, annotations, mediaType, httpHeaders, entityStream);\n    }\n\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.params;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.errors.ErrorMessage;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Nullable;\nimport javax.ws.rs.WebApplicationException;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.MediaType;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Response;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Response.Status;\n\n/**\n * An abstract base class from which to build Jersey parameter classes.\n *\n * @param <T> the type of value wrapped by the parameter\n */\npublic abstract class AbstractParam<T> {\n    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(AbstractParam.class);\n    private final String parameterName;\n    private final T value;\n\n    /**\n     * Given an input value from a client, creates a parameter wrapping its parsed value.\n     *\n     * @param input an input value from a client request, might be {@code null}\n     */\n    protected AbstractParam(@Nullable String input) {\n        this(input, \"Parameter\");\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Given an input value from a client, creates a parameter wrapping its parsed value.\n     *\n     * @param input         an input value from a client request, might be {@code null}\n     * @param parameterName name of the parameter with the provided value\n     */\n    protected AbstractParam(@Nullable String input, String parameterName) {\n        this.parameterName = parameterName;\n        try {\n            this.value = parse(input);\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            throw new WebApplicationException(error(input, e));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Given a string representation which was unable to be parsed and the exception thrown, produce\n     * a {@link Response} to be sent to the client.\n     *\n     * By default, generates a {@code 400 Bad Request} with a plain text entity generated by\n     * {@link #errorMessage(Exception)}.\n     *\n     * @param input the raw input value\n     * @param e the exception thrown while parsing {@code input}\n     * @return the {@link Response} to be sent to the client\n     */\n    protected Response error(@Nullable String input, Exception e) {\n        LOGGER.debug(\"Invalid input received: {}\", input);\n        String errorMessage = errorMessage(e);\n        if (errorMessage.contains(\"%s\")) {\n            errorMessage = String.format(errorMessage, parameterName);\n        }\n        return Response.status(getErrorStatus())\n                       .entity(new ErrorMessage(getErrorStatus().getStatusCode(),\n                               errorMessage))\n                       .type(mediaType())\n                       .build();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the media type of the error message entity.\n     *\n     * @return the media type of the error message entity\n     */\n    protected MediaType mediaType() {\n        return MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Given a string representation which was unable to be parsed and the exception thrown, produce\n     * an entity to be sent to the client.\n     *\n     * @param e the exception thrown while parsing {@code input}\n     * @return the error message to be sent the client\n     */\n    protected String errorMessage(Exception e) {\n        return String.format(\"%s is invalid: %s\", parameterName, e.getMessage());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Given a string representation which was unable to be parsed, produce a {@link Status} for the\n     * {@link Response} to be sent to the client.\n     *\n     * @return the HTTP {@link Status} of the error message\n     */\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"MethodMayBeStatic\")\n    protected Status getErrorStatus() {\n        return Status.BAD_REQUEST;\n    }\n\n   /**\n    * Given a string representation, parse it and return an instance of the parameter type.\n    *\n    * @param input the raw input\n    * @return {@code input}, parsed as an instance of {@code T}\n    * @throws Exception if there is an error parsing the input\n    */\n    protected abstract T parse(@Nullable String input) throws Exception;\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the underlying value.\n     *\n     * @return the underlying value\n     */\n    public T get() {\n        return value;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean equals(Object obj) {\n        if (this == obj) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        if ((obj == null) || (getClass() != obj.getClass())) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        final AbstractParam<?> that = (AbstractParam<?>) obj;\n        return value.equals(that.value);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public int hashCode() {\n        return value.hashCode();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n        return value.toString();\n    }\n}\n"

Please use {@link java.util.Optional} instead.\n */\n@Deprecated\npublic class SizeParam extends AbstractParam<Size> {\n\n    public SizeParam(@Nullable String input) {\n        super(input);\n    }\n\n    public SizeParam(@Nullable String input, String parameterName) {\n        super(input, parameterName);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    protected String errorMessage(Exception e) {\n        return \"%s is not a valid size.\";\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    protected Size parse(@Nullable String input) throws Exception {\n        return Size.parse(requireNonNull(input));\n    }\n}\n"

      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.validation;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.validation.ConstraintViolations;\nimport io.dropwizard.validation.Validated;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.internal.inject.ConfiguredValidator;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.model.Invocable;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.model.Parameter;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintViolation;\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintViolationException;\nimport javax.validation.Validator;\nimport javax.validation.executable.ExecutableValidator;\nimport javax.validation.groups.Default;\nimport javax.validation.metadata.BeanDescriptor;\nimport javax.ws.rs.WebApplicationException;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.HashSet;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Set;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\npublic class DropwizardConfiguredValidator implements ConfiguredValidator {\n    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(DropwizardConfiguredValidator.class);\n\n    private final Validator validator;\n\n    public DropwizardConfiguredValidator(Validator validator) {\n        this.validator = requireNonNull(validator);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void validateResourceAndInputParams(Object resource, final Invocable invocable, Object[] objects)\n            throws ConstraintViolationException {\n        final Class<?>[] groups = getGroup(invocable);\n        final Set<ConstraintViolation<Object>> violations = new HashSet<>();\n        final BeanDescriptor beanDescriptor = getConstraintsForClass(resource.getClass());\n\n        if (beanDescriptor.isBeanConstrained()) {\n            violations.addAll(validate(resource, groups));\n        }\n\n        violations.addAll(forExecutables().validateParameters(resource, invocable.getHandlingMethod(), objects, groups));\n        if (!violations.isEmpty()) {\n            throw new JerseyViolationException(violations, invocable);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * If the request entity is annotated with {@link Validated} then run\n     * validations in the specified constraint group else validate with the\n     * {@link Default} group\n     */\n    private Class<?>[] getGroup(Invocable invocable) {\n        final List<Class<?>[]> groups = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Parameter parameter : invocable.getParameters()) {\n            if (parameter.isAnnotationPresent(Validated.class)) {\n                groups.add(parameter.getAnnotation(Validated.class).value());\n            }\n        }\n\n        switch (groups.size()) {\n            // No parameters were annotated with Validated, so validate under the default group\n            case 0: return new Class<?>[] {Default.class};\n\n            // A single parameter was annotated with Validated, so use their group\n            case 1: return groups.get(0);\n\n            // Multiple parameters were annotated with Validated, so we must check if\n            // all groups are equal to each other, if not, throw an exception because\n            // the validator is unable to handle parameters validated under different\n            // groups. If the parameters have the same group, we can grab the first\n            // group.\n            default:\n                for (int i = 0; i < groups.size(); i++) {\n                    for (int j = i; j < groups.size(); j++) {\n                        if (!Arrays.deepEquals(groups.get(i), groups.get(j))) {\n                            throw new WebApplicationException(\"Parameters must have the same validation groups in \" +\n                                invocable.getHandlingMethod().getName(), 500);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                return groups.get(0);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void validateResult(Object resource, Invocable invocable, Object returnValue)\n            throws ConstraintViolationException {\n        // If the Validated annotation is on a method, then validate the response with\n        // the specified constraint group.\n        final Class<?>[] groups;\n        if (invocable.getHandlingMethod().isAnnotationPresent(Validated.class)) {\n            groups = invocable.getHandlingMethod().getAnnotation(Validated.class).value();\n        } else {\n            groups = new Class<?>[]{Default.class};\n        }\n\n        final Set<ConstraintViolation<Object>> violations =\n            forExecutables().validateReturnValue(resource, invocable.getHandlingMethod(), returnValue, groups);\n        if (!violations.isEmpty()) {\n            LOGGER.trace(\"Response validation failed: {}\", ConstraintViolations.copyOf(violations));\n            throw new JerseyViolationException(violations, invocable);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public <T> Set<ConstraintViolation<T>> validate(T t, Class<?>... classes) {\n        return validator.validate(t, classes);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public <T> Set<ConstraintViolation<T>> validateProperty(T t, String s, Class<?>... classes) {\n        return validator.validateProperty(t, s, classes);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public <T> Set<ConstraintViolation<T>> validateValue(Class<T> aClass, String s, Object o, Class<?>... classes) {\n        return validator.validateValue(aClass, s, o, classes);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public BeanDescriptor getConstraintsForClass(Class<?> aClass) {\n        return validator.getConstraintsForClass(aClass);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public <T> T unwrap(Class<T> aClass) {\n        return validator.unwrap(aClass);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public ExecutableValidator forExecutables() {\n        return validator.forExecutables();\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/validation/FuzzyEnumParamConverter.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.validation;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.errors.ErrorMessage;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Enums;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Nullable;\nimport javax.ws.rs.WebApplicationException;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.MediaType;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Response;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.ParamConverter;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.Provider;\nimport java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException;\nimport java.lang.reflect.Method;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.stream.Collectors;\n\n/**\n * Provides converters to jersey for enums used as resource parameters.\n *\n * <p>By default jersey will return a 404 if a resource parameter of an enum type cannot be converted. This class\n * provides converters for all enum types used as resource parameters that provide better error handling. If an\n * invalid value is provided for the parameter a {@code 400 Bad Request} is returned and the error message will\n * include the parameter name and a list of valid values.</p>\n *\n * @since 2.0\n */\n@Provider\npublic class FuzzyEnumParamConverter<T> implements ParamConverter<T> {\n    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(FuzzyEnumParamConverter.class);\n\n    private final Class<T> rawType;\n    private final Method fromStringMethod;\n    private final Enum<?>[] constants;\n    private final String parameterName;\n\n    FuzzyEnumParamConverter(Class<T> rawType,\n                            Method fromStringMethod,\n                            Enum<?>[] constants,\n                            String parameterName) {\n        this.rawType = rawType;\n        this.fromStringMethod = fromStringMethod;\n        this.constants = constants;\n        this.parameterName = parameterName;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    @Nullable\n    public T fromString(String value) {\n        if (value == null || value.isEmpty()) {\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        if (fromStringMethod != null) {\n            try {\n                Object constant = fromStringMethod.invoke(null, value);\n                // return if a value is found\n                if (constant != null) {\n                    @SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\") final T returnValue = (T) constant;\n                    return returnValue;\n                }\n                final String errMsg = String.format(\"%s is not a valid %s\", parameterName, rawType.getSimpleName());\n                throw new WebApplicationException(getErrorResponse(errMsg));\n            } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {\n                LOGGER.debug(\"Not permitted to call fromString on {}\", rawType.getSimpleName(), e);\n                throw new WebApplicationException(getErrorResponse(\"Not permitted to call fromString on %s\" + rawType.getSimpleName()));\n            } catch (InvocationTargetException e) {\n                if (e.getCause() instanceof WebApplicationException) {\n                    throw (WebApplicationException) e.getCause();\n                }\n                LOGGER.debug(\"Failed to convert {} to {}\", parameterName, rawType.getSimpleName(), e);\n                throw new WebApplicationException(getErrorResponse(\"Failed to convert \" + parameterName + \" to \" + rawType.getSimpleName()));\n            }\n        }\n\n        Object constant = Enums.fromStringFuzzy(value, constants);\n\n        // return if a value is found\n        if (constant != null) {\n            @SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\") final T returnValue = (T) constant;\n            return returnValue;\n        }\n\n        final String constantsList = Arrays.stream(constants)\n                .map(Enum::toString)\n                .collect(Collectors.joining(\", \"));\n        final String errMsg = String.format(\"%s must be one of [%s]\", parameterName, constantsList);\n        throw new WebApplicationException(getErrorResponse(errMsg));\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString(T value) {\n        return value.toString();\n    }\n\n    private Response getErrorResponse(String message) {\n        return Response\n                .status(400)\n                .entity(new ErrorMessage(400, message))\n                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)\n                .build();\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.validation;\n\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.internal.inject.AbstractBinder;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.internal.inject.ConfiguredValidator;\n\nimport javax.validation.Validator;\n\n/**\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic class HibernateValidationBinder extends AbstractBinder {\n    private final Validator validator;\n\n    public HibernateValidationBinder(Validator validator) {\n        this.validator = validator;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    protected void configure() {\n        bind(new DropwizardConfiguredValidator(validator)).to(ConfiguredValidator.class);\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/validation/JerseyParameterNameProvider.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.validation;\n\nimport org.hibernate.validator.parameternameprovider.ReflectionParameterNameProvider;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.QueryParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.PathParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.HeaderParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.CookieParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.FormParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.MatrixParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Context;\nimport java.lang.annotation.Annotation;\nimport java.lang.reflect.Method;\nimport java.lang.reflect.Parameter;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Optional;\n\n/**\n * Adds jersey support to parameter name discovery in hibernate validator.\n *\n * <p>This provider will behave like the hibernate-provided {@link ReflectionParameterNameProvider} except when a\n * method parameter is annotated with a jersey parameter annotation, like {@link QueryParam}. If a jersey parameter\n * annotation is present the value of the annotation is used as the parameter name.</p>\n */\npublic class JerseyParameterNameProvider extends ReflectionParameterNameProvider {\n\n    @Override\n    public List<String> getParameterNames(Method method) {\n        Parameter[] parameters = method.getParameters();\n        Annotation[][] parameterAnnotations = method.getParameterAnnotations();\n        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>(parameterAnnotations.length);\n        for (int i = 0; i < parameterAnnotations.length; i++) {\n            Annotation[] annotations = parameterAnnotations[i];\n            String name = getParameterNameFromAnnotations(annotations).orElse(parameters[i].getName());\n            names.add(name);\n        }\n        return names;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Derives member's name and type from it's annotations\n     */\n    public static Optional<String> getParameterNameFromAnnotations(Annotation[] memberAnnotations) {\n        for (Annotation a : memberAnnotations) {\n            if (a instanceof QueryParam) {\n                return Optional.of(\"query param \" + ((QueryParam) a).value());\n            } else if (a instanceof PathParam) {\n                return Optional.of(\"path param \" + ((PathParam) a).value());\n            } else if (a instanceof HeaderParam) {\n                return Optional.of(\"header \" + ((HeaderParam) a).value());\n            } else if (a instanceof CookieParam) {\n                return Optional.of(\"cookie \" + ((CookieParam) a).value());\n            } else if (a instanceof FormParam) {\n                return Optional.of(\"form field \" + ((FormParam) a).value());\n            } else if (a instanceof Context) {\n                return Optional.of(\"context\");\n            } else if (a instanceof MatrixParam) {\n                return Optional.of(\"matrix param \" + ((MatrixParam) a).value());\n            }\n        }\n\n        return Optional.empty();\n    }\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/validation/JerseyViolationExceptionMapper.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.validation;\n\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.server.model.Invocable;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintViolation;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Response;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;\nimport javax.ws.rs.ext.Provider;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport java.util.stream.Collectors;\n\n@Provider\npublic class JerseyViolationExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<JerseyViolationException> {\n    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(JerseyViolationExceptionMapper.class);\n\n    @Override\n    public Response toResponse(final JerseyViolationException exception) {\n        // Provide a way to log if desired, Issue #2128, PR #2129\n        LOGGER.debug(\"Object validation failure\", exception);\n\n        final Set<ConstraintViolation<?>> violations = exception.getConstraintViolations();\n        final Invocable invocable = exception.getInvocable();\n        final List<String> errors = exception.getConstraintViolations().stream()\n                .map(violation -> ConstraintMessage.getMessage(violation, invocable))\n                .collect(Collectors.toList());\n\n        final int status = ConstraintMessage.determineStatus(violations, invocable);\n        return Response.status(status)\n                .entity(new ValidationErrorMessage(errors))\n                .build();\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.validation;\n\nimport org.hibernate.validator.internal.engine.constraintvalidation.ConstraintValidatorFactoryImpl;\n\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintValidator;\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintValidatorFactory;\n\n/**\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic class MutableValidatorFactory implements ConstraintValidatorFactory {\n\n    private ConstraintValidatorFactory validatorFactory = new ConstraintValidatorFactoryImpl();\n\n    @Override\n    public final <T extends ConstraintValidator<?, ?>> T getInstance(Class<T> key) {\n        return validatorFactory.getInstance(key);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void releaseInstance(ConstraintValidator<?, ?> instance) { }\n\n    public void setValidatorFactory(ConstraintValidatorFactory validatorFactory) {\n        this.validatorFactory = validatorFactory;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
