io.dropwizard.jersey.DropwizardResourceConfig;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.MyMessage;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.MyMessageParamConverterProvider;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.params.UUIDParam;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.BadRequestException;\nimport javax.ws.rs.CookieParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.GET;\nimport javax.ws.rs.Path;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Application;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\n\nclass OptionalCookieParamResourceTest extends AbstractJerseyTest {\n\n    @Override\n    protected Application configure() {\n        return DropwizardResourceConfig.forTesting()\n                .register(OptionalCookieParamResource.class)\n                .register(MyMessageParamConverterProvider.class);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnDefaultMessageWhenMessageIsNotPresent() {\n        String defaultMessage = \"Default Message\";\n        String response = target(\"/optional/message\").request().get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEqualTo(defaultMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnMessageWhenMessageIsBlank() {\n        String response = target(\"/optional/message\").request().cookie(\"message\", \"\").get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnMessageWhenMessageIsPresent() {\n        String customMessage = \"Custom Message\";\n        String response = target(\"/optional/message\").request().cookie(\"message\", customMessage).get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEqualTo(customMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnDefaultMessageWhenMyMessageIsNotPresent() {\n        String defaultMessage = \"My Default Message\";\n        String response = target(\"/optional/my-message\").request().get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEqualTo(defaultMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnMyMessageWhenMyMessageIsPresent() {\n uuid = \"fd94b00d-bd50-46b3-b42f-905a9c9e7d78\";\n        String response = target(\"/optional/uuid\").request().cookie(\"uuid\", uuid).get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEqualTo(uuid);\n    }\n\n    @Path(\"/optional\")\n    public static class OptionalCookieParamResource {\n        @GET\n        @Path(\"/message\")\n        public String getMessage(@CookieParam(\"message\") Optional<String> message) {\n            return message.or(\"Default Message\");\n        }\n\n        @GET\n        @Path(\"/my-message\")\n        public String getMyMessage(@CookieParam(\"mymessage\") Optional<MyMessage> myMessage) {\n            return myMessage.or(new MyMessage(\"My Default Message\")).getMessage();\n        }\n\n        @GET\n        @Path(\"/uuid\")\n        public String getUUID(@CookieParam(\"uuid\") Optional<UUIDParam> uuid) {\n            return uuid.or(new UUIDParam(\"d5672fa8-326b-40f6-bf71-d9dacf44bcdc\")).get().toString();\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

  assertThat(response.readEntity(String.class)).isEqualTo(uuid);\n    }\n\n    @Path(\"/optional\")\n    public static class OptionalFormParamResource {\n\n        @POST\n        @Path(\"/message\")\n        public String getMessage(@FormParam(\"message\") Optional<String> message) {\n            return message.or(\"Default Message\");\n        }\n\n        @POST\n        @Path(\"/my-message\")\n        public String getMyMessage(@FormParam(\"mymessage\") Optional<MyMessage> myMessage) {\n            return myMessage.or(new MyMessage(\"My Default Message\")).getMessage();\n        }\n\n        @POST\n        @Path(\"/uuid\")\n        public String getUUID(@FormParam(\"uuid\") Optional<UUIDParam> uuid) {\n            return uuid.or(new io.dropwizard.jersey.DropwizardResourceConfig;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.MyMessage;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.MyMessageParamConverterProvider;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.params.UUIDParam;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.BadRequestException;\nimport javax.ws.rs.GET;\nimport javax.ws.rs.HeaderParam;\nimport assertThat(response).isEqualTo(uuid);\n    }\n\n    @Path(\"/optional\")\n    public static class OptionalHeaderParamResource {\n        @GET\n        @Path(\"/message\")\n        public String javax.ws.rs.GET;\nimport javax.ws.rs.POST;\nimport javax.ws.rs.Path;\nimport javax.ws.rs.Produces;\nimport javax.ws.rs.QueryParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.WebApplicationException;\nimport org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\n\nclass OptionalMessageBodyWriterTest extends AbstractJerseyTest {\n\n    @Override\n    protected Application configure() {\n        return DropwizardResourceConfig.forTesting()\n                .register(new EmptyOptionalExceptionMapper())\n                .register(OptionalReturnResource.class);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void presentOptionalsReturnTheirValue() {\n        assertThat(target(\"/optional-return/\")\n                .queryParam(\"id\", \"woo\").request()\n                .get(String.class))\n                .isEqualTo(\"woo\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void absentOptionalsThrowANotFound() {\n        Invocation.Builder request = target(\"/optional-return/\").request();\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(WebApplicationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> request.get(String.class))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(404));\n    }\n\n    @Path(\"/optional-return/\")\n    @Produces(MediaType.TEXT_PLAIN)\n    public static class OptionalReturnResource {\n        @GET\n        public Optional<String> showWithQueryParam(@QueryParam(\"id\") String id) {\n            return Optional.fromNullable(id);\n        }\n\n        @POST\n        public Optional<String> showWithFormParam(@FormParam(\"id\") String id) {\n            return Optional.fromNullable(id);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.optional;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.AbstractJerseyTest;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.DropwizardResourceConfig;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.DefaultValue;\nimport javax.ws.rs.FormParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.GET;\nimport javax.ws.rs.NotFoundException;\nimport javax.ws.rs.POST;\nimport javax.ws.rs.Path;\nimport javax.ws.rs.Produces;\nimport javax.ws.rs.QueryParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.WebApplicationException;\nimport javax.ws.rs.client.Invocation;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Application;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.MediaType;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Response;\nimport java.util.OptionalDouble;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\n\nclass OptionalDoubleMessageBodyWriterTest extends AbstractJerseyTest {\n\n    @Override\n    protected Application configure() {\n        return DropwizardResourceConfig.forTesting()\n                    .register(new EmptyOptionalExceptionMapper())\n                    .register(OptionalDoubleReturnResource.class);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void presentOptionalsReturnTheirValue() {\n        assertThat(target(\"optional-return\")\n                .queryParam(\"id\", \"1\").request()\n                .get(Double.class))\n                .isEqualTo(1);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void presentOptionalsReturnTheirValueWithResponse() {\n        assertThat(target(\"optional-return/response-wrapped\")\n                .queryParam(\"id\", \"1\").request()\n                .get(Double.class))\n                .isEqualTo(1);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void absentOptionalsThrowANotFound() {\n        Invocation.Builder request = target(\"optional-return\").request();\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(WebApplicationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> request.get(Double.class))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(404));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void valueSetIgnoresDefault() {\n        assertThat(target(\"optional-return/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"1\").request().get(Double.class))\n            .isEqualTo(target(\"optional-return/double/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"1\").request().get(Double.class))\n            .isEqualTo(1);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void valueNotSetReturnsDefault() {\n        assertThat(target(\"optional-return/default\").request().get(Double.class))\n            .isEqualTo(target(\"optional-return/double/default\").request().get(Double.class))\n            .isEqualTo(0);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void valueEmptyReturnsDefault() {\n        assertThat(target(\"optional-return/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"\")\n            .request().get(Double.class))\n            .isEqualTo(target(\"optional-return/double/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"\")\n                .request().get(Double.class))\n            .isEqualTo(0);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void valueInvalidReturns404() {\n        Invocation.Builder request = target(\"optional-return/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"invalid\")\n            .request();\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(NotFoundException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> request.get(Double.class));\n        Invocation.Builder doubleRequest = target(\"optional-return/double/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"invalid\")\n            .request();\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(NotFoundException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> doubleRequest.get(Double.class));\n    }\n\n    @Path(\"optional-return\")\n    @Produces(MediaType.TEXT_PLAIN)\n    public static class OptionalDoubleReturnResource {\n        @GET\n        public OptionalDouble showWithQueryParam(@QueryParam(\"id\") OptionalDouble id) {\n            return id;\n        }\n\n        @POST\n        public OptionalDouble showWithFormParam(@FormParam(\"id\") OptionalDouble id) {\n            return id;\n        }\n\n        @Path(\"response-wrapped\")\n        @GET\n        public Response showWithQueryParamResponse(@QueryParam(\"id\") OptionalDouble id) {\n            return Response.ok(id).build();\n        }\n\n        @Path(\"default\")\n        @GET\n        public OptionalDouble showWithQueryParamAndDefaultValue(@QueryParam(\"id\") @DefaultValue(\"0\") OptionalDouble id) {\n            return id;\n        }\n\n        @Path(\"double/default\")\n        @GET\n        public Double showWithLongQueryParamAndDefaultValue(@QueryParam(\"id\") @DefaultValue(\"0\") Double id) {\n            return id;\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

io.dropwizard.jersey.MyMessage;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.MyMessageParamConverterProvider;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.params.UUIDParam;\nimport org.glassfish.jersey.internal.util.collection.MultivaluedStringMap;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.FormParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.POST;\nimport javax.ws.rs.Path;\nimport javax.ws.rs.client.Entity;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Application;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Form;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Response;\nimport java.util.Optional;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\n\nclass OptionalFormParamResourceTest extends AbstractJerseyTest {\n\n    @Override\n    protected Application configure() {\n        return DropwizardResourceConfig.forTesting()\n                .register(OptionalFormParamResource.class)\n                .register(MyMessageParamConverterProvider.class);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnDefaultMessageWhenMessageIsNotPresent() {\n        final String defaultMessage = \"Default Message\";\n        final Response response = target(\"/optional/message\").request().post(Entity.form(new MultivaluedStringMap()));\n\n        assertThat(response.readEntity(String.class)).isEqualTo(defaultMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnMessageWhenMessageBlank() {\n        final Form form = new Form(\"message\", \"\");\n        final Response response = target(\"/optional/message\").request().post(Entity.form(form));\n\n        assertThat(response.readEntity(String.class)).isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnMessageWhenMessageIsPresent() {\n        final String customMessage = \"Custom Message\";\n        final Form form = new Form(\"message\", customMessage);\n        final Response response = target(\"/optional/message\").request().post(Entity.form(form));\n\n        assertThat(response.readEntity(String.class)).isEqualTo(customMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnDefaultMessageWhenMyMessageIsNotPresent() {\n        final String defaultMessage = \"My Default Message\";\n        final Response response = target(\"/optional/my-message\").request().post(Entity.form(new MultivaluedStringMap()));\n\n        assertThat(response.readEntity(String.class)).isEqualTo(defaultMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnMyMessageWhenMyMessageIsPresent() {\n        final String myMessage = \"My Message\";\n        final Form form = new Form(\"mymessage\", myMessage);\n        final Response response = target(\"/optional/my-message\").request().post(Entity.form(form));\n\n        assertThat(response.readEntity(String.class)).isEqualTo(myMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldThrowBadRequestExceptionWhenInvalidUUIDIsPresent() {\n        final String invalidUUID = \"invalid-uuid\";\n        final Form form = new Form(\"uuid\", invalidUUID);\n        final Response response = target(\"/optional/uuid\").request().post(Entity.form(form));\n\n        assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST.getStatusCode());\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnDefaultUUIDWhenUUIDIsNotPresent() {\n        final String defaultUUID = \"d5672fa8-326b-40f6-bf71-d9dacf44bcdc\";\n        final Response response = target(\"/optional/uuid\").request().post(Entity.form(new MultivaluedStringMap()));\n\n        assertThat(response.readEntity(String.class)).isEqualTo(defaultUUID);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnUUIDWhenValidUUIDIsPresent() {\n        final String uuid = \"fd94b00d-bd50-46b3-b42f-905a9c9e7d78\";\n        final Form form = new Form(\"uuid\", uuid);\n        final Response response = target(\"/optional/uuid\").request().post(Entity.form(form));\n\n        assertThat(response.readEntity(String.class)).isEqualTo(uuid);\n    }\n\n    @Path(\"/optional\")\n    public static class OptionalFormParamResource {\n\n        @POST\n        @Path(\"/message\")\n        public String getMessage(@FormParam(\"message\") Optional<String> message) {\n            return message.orElse(\"Default Message\");\n        }\n\n        @POST\n        @Path(\"/my-message\")\n        public String getMyMessage(@FormParam(\"mymessage\") Optional<MyMessage> myMessage) {\n            return myMessage.orElse(new MyMessage(\"My Default Message\")).getMessage();\n        }\n\n        @POST\n        @Path(\"/uuid\")\n        public String getUUID(@FormParam(\"uuid\") Optional<UUIDParam> uuid) {\n            return uuid.orElse(new UUIDParam(\"d5672fa8-326b-40f6-bf71-d9dacf44bcdc\")).get().toString();\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.DefaultValue;\nimport javax.ws.rs.FormParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.GET;\nimport javax.ws.rs.NotFoundException;\nimport javax.ws.rs.POST;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Application;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.MediaType;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Response;\nimport java.util.OptionalInt;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\n\nclass OptionalIntMessageBodyWriterTest extends AbstractJerseyTest {\n\n    @Override\n    protected Application configure() {\n        return DropwizardResourceConfig.forTesting()\n                .register(new EmptyOptionalExceptionMapper())\n                .register(OptionalIntReturnResource.class);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void presentOptionalsReturnTheirValue() {\n        assertThat(target(\"optional-return\")\n                .queryParam(\"id\", \"1\").request()\n                .get(Integer.class))\n                .isEqualTo(1);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void presentOptionalsReturnTheirValueWithResponse() {\n        assertThat(target(\"optional-return/response-wrapped\")\n                .queryParam(\"id\", \"1\").request()\n                .get(Integer.class))\n                .isEqualTo(1);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void absentOptionalsThrowANotFound() {\n        Invocation.Builder request = target(\"optional-return\").request();\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(WebApplicationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> request.get(Integer.class))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(404));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void valueSetIgnoresDefault() {\n        assertThat(target(\"optional-return/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"1\")\n            .request().get(Integer.class))\n            .isEqualTo(target(\"optional-return/int/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"1\")\n                .request().get(Integer.class))\n            .isEqualTo(1);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void valueNotSetReturnsDefault() {\n        assertThat(target(\"optional-return/default\").request().get(Integer.class))\n            .isEqualTo(target(\"optional-return/int/default\").request().get(Integer.class))\n            .isEqualTo(0);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void valueEmptyReturnsDefault() {\n        assertThat(target(\"optional-return/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"\")\n            .request().get(Integer.class))\n            .isEqualTo(target(\"optional-return/int/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"\")\n                .request().get(Integer.class))\n            .isEqualTo(0);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void valueInvalidReturns404() {\n        Invocation.Builder request = target(\"optional-return/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"invalid\")\n            .request();\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(NotFoundException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> request.get(Integer.class));\n        Invocation.Builder intRequest = target(\"optional-return/int/default\").queryParam(\"id\", \"invalid\")\n            .request();\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(NotFoundException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> intRequest.get(Integer.class));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void verifyInvalidDefaultValueFailsFast() {\n        OptionalIntParamConverterProvider.OptionalIntParamConverter converter =\n            new OptionalIntParamConverterProvider.OptionalIntParamConverter(\"invalid\");\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(NumberFormatException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> converter.fromString(\"invalid\"));\n    }\n\n    @Path(\"optional-return\")\n    @Produces(MediaType.TEXT_PLAIN)\n    public static class OptionalIntReturnResource {\n        @GET\n        public OptionalInt showWithQueryParam(@QueryParam(\"id\") OptionalInt id) {\n            return id;\n        }\n\n        @POST\n        public OptionalInt showWithFormParam(@FormParam(\"id\") OptionalInt id) {\n            return id;\n        }\n\n        @Path(\"response-wrapped\")\n        @GET\n        public Response showWithQueryParamResponse(@QueryParam(\"id\") OptionalInt id) {\n            return Response.ok(id).build();\n        }\n\n        @Path(\"default\")\n        @GET\n        public OptionalInt showWithQueryParamAndDefaultValue(@QueryParam(\"id\") @DefaultValue(\"0\") OptionalInt id) {\n            return id;\n        }\n\n        @Path(\"int/default\")\n        @GET\n        public Integer showWithLongQueryParamAndDefaultValue(@QueryParam(\"id\") @DefaultValue(\"0\") Integer id) {\n            return id;\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

       assertThatExceptionOfType(WebApplicationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> request.get(String.class))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(404));\n    }\n\n    @Path(\"optional-return\")\n    @Produces(MediaType.TEXT_PLAIN)\n    public static class OptionalReturnResource {\n        @GET\n io.dropwizard.jersey.MyMessage;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.MyMessageParamConverterProvider;\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.params.UUIDParam;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.BadRequestException;\nimport javax.ws.rs.GET;\nimport javax.ws.rs.Path;\nimport javax.ws.rs.QueryParam;\nimport javax.ws.rs.core.Application;\nimport java.util.Optional;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\n\nclass OptionalQueryParamResourceTest extends AbstractJerseyTest {\n\n    @Override\n    protected Application configure() {\n        return DropwizardResourceConfig.forTesting()\n                .register(OptionalQueryParamResource.class)\n                .register(MyMessageParamConverterProvider.class);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnDefaultMessageWhenMessageIsNotPresent() {\n        String defaultMessage = \"Default Message\";\n        String response = target(\"/optional/message\").request().get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEqualTo(defaultMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnMessageWhenMessageIsPresent() {\n        String customMessage = \"Custom Message\";\n        String response = target(\"/optional/message\").queryParam(\"message\", customMessage).request().get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEqualTo(customMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnMessageWhenMessageIsBlank() {\n        String response = target(\"/optional/message\").queryParam(\"message\", \"\").request().get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnDecodedMessageWhenEncodedMessageIsPresent() {\n        String encodedMessage = \"Custom%20Message\";\n        String decodedMessage = \"Custom Message\";\n        String response = target(\"/optional/message\").queryParam(\"message\", encodedMessage).request().get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEqualTo(decodedMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnDefaultMessageWhenMyMessageIsNotPresent() {\n        String defaultMessage = \"My Default Message\";\n        String response = target(\"/optional/my-message\").request().get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEqualTo(defaultMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnMyMessageWhenMyMessageIsPresent() {\n        String myMessage = \"My Message\";\n        String response = target(\"/optional/my-message\").queryParam(\"mymessage\", myMessage).request().get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEqualTo(myMessage);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldThrowBadRequestExceptionWhenInvalidUUIDIsPresent() {\n        String invalidUUID = \"invalid-uuid\";\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(BadRequestException.class).isThrownBy(() ->\n            target(\"/optional/uuid\").queryParam(\"uuid\", invalidUUID).request().get(String.class));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnDefaultUUIDWhenUUIDIsNotPresent() {\n        String defaultUUID = \"d5672fa8-326b-40f6-bf71-d9dacf44bcdc\";\n        String response = target(\"/optional/uuid\").request().get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEqualTo(defaultUUID);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldReturnUUIDWhenValidUUIDIsPresent() {\n        String uuid = \"fd94b00d-bd50-46b3-b42f-905a9c9e7d78\";\n        String response = target(\"/optional/uuid\").queryParam(\"uuid\", uuid).request().get(String.class);\n        assertThat(response).isEqualTo(uuid);\n    }\n\n    @Path(\"/optional\")\n    public static class OptionalQueryParamResource {\n\n        @GET\n        @Path(\"/message\")\n        public String getMessage(@QueryParam(\"message\") Optional<String> message) {\n            return message.orElse(\"Default Message\");\n        }\n\n        @GET\n        @Path(\"/my-message\")\n        public String getMyMessage(@QueryParam(\"mymessage\") Optional<MyMessage> myMessage) {\n            return myMessage.orElse(new MyMessage(\"My Default Message\")).getMessage();\n        }\n\n        @GET\n        @Path(\"/uuid\")\n        public String getUUID(@QueryParam(\"uuid\") Optional<UUIDParam> uuid) {\n            return uuid.orElse(new       "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/params/BooleanParamTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.params;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.errors.ErrorMessage;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Nullable;\nimport javax.ws.rs.WebApplicationException;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\n\nclass BooleanParamTest {\n\n    @Test\n    void trueReturnsTrue() {\n        assertThat(new BooleanParam(\"true\").get()).isTrue();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void uppercaseTrueReturnsTrue() {\n        assertThat(new BooleanParam(\"TRUE\").get()).isTrue();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void falseReturnsFalse() {\n        assertThat(new BooleanParam(\"false\").get()).isFalse();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void uppercaseFalseReturnsFalse() {\n        assertThat(new BooleanParam(\"FALSE\").get()).isFalse();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void nullThrowsAnException() {\n        booleanParamNegativeTest(null);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void nonBooleanValuesThrowAnException() {\n        booleanParamNegativeTest(\"foo\");\n    }\n\n    private void booleanParamNegativeTest(@Nullable String input) {\n        final ErrorMessage expected = new ErrorMessage(400, \"Parameter must be \\\"true\\\" or \\\"false\\\".\");\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(WebApplicationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> new BooleanParam(input))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(400))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getEntity()).isEqualTo(expected));\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/params/DateTimeParamTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.params;\n\nimport org.joda.time.DateTime;\nimport org.joda.time.DateTimeZone;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\n\nclass DateTimeParamTest {\n    @Test\n    void parsesDateTimes() {\n        final DateTimeParam param = new DateTimeParam(\"2012-11-19\");\n\n        assertThat(param.get())\n                .isEqualTo(new DateTime(2012, 11, 19, 0, 0, DateTimeZone.UTC));\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/params/DurationParamTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.params;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.errors.ErrorMessage;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Duration;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.WebApplicationException;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\n\nclass DurationParamTest {\n\n    @Test\n    void parseDurationSeconds() {\n        assertThat(new DurationParam(\"10 seconds\").get())\n            .isEqualTo(Duration.seconds(10));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void badValueThrowsException() {\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(WebApplicationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> new DurationParam(\"invalid\", \"param_name\"))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(400))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getEntity()).isEqualTo(\n                    new ErrorMessage(400, \"param_name is not a valid duration.\")\n                ));\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "dropwizard-jersey/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/jersey/params/InstantParamTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.params;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.errors.ErrorMessage;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport java.time.Instant;\nimport java.time.ZoneOffset;\nimport java.time.LocalDateTime;\nimport javax.ws.rs.WebApplicationException;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\n\nclass InstantParamTest {\n    @Test\n    void parsesDateTimes() {\n        final InstantParam param = new InstantParam(\"2012-11-19T00:00:00Z\");\n        Instant instant = LocalDateTime.of(2012, 11, 19, 0, 0)\n            .toInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC);\n\n        assertThat(param.get())\n            .isEqualTo(instant);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void nullThrowsAnException() {\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(WebApplicationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> new InstantParam(null, \"myDate\"))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(400))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getEntity()).isEqualTo(\n                new ErrorMessage(400, \"myDate must be in a ISO-8601 format.\")\n            ));\n    }\n}\n"
    },

static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\n\nclass IntParamTest {\n    @Test\n    void anIntegerReturnsAnInteger() {\n        assertThat(new IntParam(\"200\").get()).isEqualTo(200);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void nullThrowsAnException() {\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(WebApplicationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> new IntParam(null))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(400))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getEntity()).isEqualTo(\n                new ErrorMessage(400, \"Parameter is not a number.\")\n            ));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void emptyStringThrowsAnException() {\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(WebApplicationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> new IntParam(\"\"))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(400))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getEntity()).isEqualTo(\n                new ErrorMessage(400, \"Parameter is not a number.\")\n            ));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void aNonIntegerThrowsAnException() {\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(WebApplicationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> new IntParam(\"foo\"))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(400))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getEntity()).isEqualTo(\n       "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.jersey.params;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.jersey.errors.ErrorMessage;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.ws.rs.WebApplicationException;\nimport java.util.UUID;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\n\nclass UUIDParamTest {\n    private void UuidParamNegativeTest(String input) {\n        assertThatExceptionOfType(WebApplicationException.class)\n            .isThrownBy(() -> new UUIDParam(input))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(400))\n            .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getResponse().getEntity()).isEqualTo(\n                new ErrorMessage(400, \"Parameter is not a UUID.\")\n            ));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void aUUIDStringReturnsAUUIDObject() {\n        final String uuidString = \"067e6162-3b6f-4ae2-a171-2470b63dff00\";\n        final UUID uuid = UUID.fromString(uuidString);\n\n        assertThat(new UUIDParam(uuidString).get()).isEqualTo(uuid);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void noSpaceUUID() {\n        UuidParamNegativeTest(\"067e61623b6f4ae2a1712470b63dff00\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void tooLongUUID() {\n        UuidParamNegativeTest(\"067e6162-3b6f-4ae2-a171-2470b63dff000\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void aNonUUIDThrowsAnException() {\n        UuidParamNegativeTest(\"foo\");\n    }\n}\n"
    },

Unwrapping.Skip.class)\n        @Min(3)\n        IntParam inter = new IntParam(\"4\");\n\n        @NotNull(payload = Unwrapping.Skip.class)\n        @NotEmpty\n        @Length(max = 3)\n        public NonEmptyStringParam name = new NonEmptyStringParam(\"a\");\n    }\n\n    private final Validator validator = Validators.newValidator();\n\n    @Test\n    void succeedsWithAllGoodData() {\n        final Example example = new Example();\n        final Set<ConstraintViolation<Example>> validate = validator.validate(example);\n        assertThat(validate).isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void @SuppressWarnings(\"NullAway\")\n    @Test\n    void failsWithNullNonEmptyStringParam() {\n        final Example example = new Example();\n        example.name = null;\n        final assertThat(validate).hasSize(1);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void failsWithEmptyNonEmptyStringParam() {\n        final Example example = new Example();\n        example.name = new NonEmptyStringParam(\"\");\n        final Set<ConstraintViolation<Example>> validate = validator.validate(example);\n        assertThat(validate).hasSize(1);\n    }\n}\n"

      "path": "dropwizard-lifecycle/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/lifecycle/ExecutorServiceManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.lifecycle;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Duration;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;\n\npublic class ExecutorServiceManager implements Managed {\n\n    private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ExecutorServiceManager.class);\n    private final ExecutorService executor;\n    private final Duration shutdownPeriod;\n    private final String poolName;\n\n    public ExecutorServiceManager(ExecutorService executor, Duration shutdownPeriod, String poolName) {\n        this.executor = executor;\n        this.shutdownPeriod = shutdownPeriod;\n        this.poolName = poolName;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void start() throws Exception {\n        // OK BOSS\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * {@inheritDoc}\n     *\n     * @throws InterruptedException\n     *             This is thrown if the thread executing this method is\n     *             interrupted while awaiting executor tasks to complete.\n     */\n    @Override\n    public void stop() throws InterruptedException, Exception {\n        executor.shutdown();\n        final boolean success = executor.awaitTermination(shutdownPeriod.getQuantity(), shutdownPeriod.getUnit());\n        if (!success && LOG.isDebugEnabled()) {\n            LOG.debug(\"Timeout has elapsed before termination completed for executor {}\", executor);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n        return super.toString() + '(' + poolName + ')';\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorService getExecutor() {\n        return executor;\n    }\n\n    public Duration getShutdownPeriod() {\n        return shutdownPeriod;\n    }\n\n    public String getPoolName() {\n        return poolName;\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-lifecycle/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/lifecycle/PortDescriptor.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.lifecycle;\n\nimport java.util.StringJoiner;\n\n/**\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic final class PortDescriptor {\n\n    private static final String UNKNOWN = \"UNKNOWN\";\n    private static final String DEFAULT_HOST = \"0.0.0.0\";\n\n    private final String host;\n\n    private final String protocol;\n\n    private final int port;\n\n    private final String connectorType;\n\n    public PortDescriptor() {\n        this(UNKNOWN, 0, UNKNOWN, DEFAULT_HOST);\n    }\n\n    public PortDescriptor(String protocol, int port, String connectorType, String host) {\n        this.protocol = protocol;\n        this.port = port;\n        this.connectorType = connectorType;\n        this.host = host;\n    }\n\n    public String getProtocol() {\n        return protocol;\n    }\n\n    public int getPort() {\n        return port;\n    }\n\n    public String getConnectorType() {\n        return connectorType;\n    }\n\n    public String getHost() {\n        return host;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n        return new StringJoiner(\", \", PortDescriptor.class.getSimpleName() + \"[\", \"]\")\n            .add(\"host='\" + host + \"'\")\n            .add(\"protocol='\" + protocol + \"'\")\n            .add(\"port=\" + port)\n            .add(\"connectorType='\" + connectorType + \"'\")\n            .toString();\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "dropwizard-lifecycle/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/lifecycle/setup/ExecutorServiceBuilder.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.lifecycle.setup;\n\nimport com.codahale.metrics.InstrumentedThreadFactory;\nimport io.dropwizard.lifecycle.ExecutorServiceManager;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Duration;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Nonnull;\nimport java.util.Formatter;\nimport java.util.Locale;\nimport java.util.concurrent.BlockingQueue;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;\nimport java.util.concurrent.Executors;\nimport java.util.concurrent.LinkedBlockingQueue;\nimport java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionHandler;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ThreadFactory;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ThreadPoolExecutor;\nimport java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;\n\npublic class ExecutorServiceBuilder {\n    private static Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ExecutorServiceBuilder.class);\n\n    private static final AtomicLong COUNT = new AtomicLong(0);\n    private final LifecycleEnvironment environment;\n    @Nonnull\n    private final String nameFormat;\n    private int corePoolSize;\n    private int maximumPoolSize;\n    private boolean allowCoreThreadTimeOut;\n    private Duration keepAliveTime;\n    private Duration shutdownTime;\n    private BlockingQueue<Runnable> workQueue;\n    private ThreadFactory threadFactory;\n    private RejectedExecutionHandler handler;\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder(LifecycleEnvironment environment, String nameFormat, ThreadFactory factory) {\n        this.environment = environment;\n        this.nameFormat = nameFormat;\n        this.corePoolSize = 0;\n        this.maximumPoolSize = 1;\n        this.allowCoreThreadTimeOut = false;\n        this.keepAliveTime = Duration.seconds(60);\n        this.shutdownTime = Duration.seconds(5);\n        this.workQueue = new LinkedBlockingQueue<>();\n        this.threadFactory = factory;\n        this.handler = new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy();\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder(LifecycleEnvironment environment, String nameFormat) {\n        this(environment, nameFormat, buildThreadFactory(nameFormat));\n    }\n\n    private static ThreadFactory buildThreadFactory(String nameFormat) {\n        ThreadFactory defaultThreadFactory = Executors.defaultThreadFactory();\n\n        // Validate the format string\n        try (Formatter fmt = new Formatter()) {\n            fmt.format(Locale.ROOT, nameFormat, 0);\n        }\n\n        return r -> {\n            final Thread thread = defaultThreadFactory.newThread(r);\n            thread.setName(String.format(Locale.ROOT, nameFormat, COUNT.incrementAndGet()));\n            return thread;\n        };\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder minThreads(int threads) {\n        this.corePoolSize = threads;\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder maxThreads(int threads) {\n        this.maximumPoolSize = threads;\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder allowCoreThreadTimeOut(boolean allowCoreThreadTimeOut) {\n        this.allowCoreThreadTimeOut = allowCoreThreadTimeOut;\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder keepAliveTime(Duration time) {\n        this.keepAliveTime = time;\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder shutdownTime(Duration time) {\n        this.shutdownTime = time;\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder workQueue(BlockingQueue<Runnable> workQueue) {\n        this.workQueue = workQueue;\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder rejectedExecutionHandler(RejectedExecutionHandler handler) {\n        this.handler = handler;\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder threadFactory(ThreadFactory threadFactory) {\n        this.threadFactory = threadFactory;\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorService build() {\n        if (corePoolSize != maximumPoolSize && maximumPoolSize > 1 && !isBoundedQueue()) {\n            log.warn(\"Parameter 'maximumPoolSize' is conflicting with unbounded work queues\");\n        }\n\n        final String nameWithoutFormat = getNameWithoutFormat(nameFormat);\n        final ThreadFactory instrumentedThreadFactory = new InstrumentedThreadFactory(threadFactory, environment.getMetricRegistry(), nameWithoutFormat);\n        final ThreadPoolExecutor executor = new ThreadPoolExecutor(corePoolSize,\n                                                                   maximumPoolSize,\n                                                                   keepAliveTime.getQuantity(),\n                                                                   keepAliveTime.getUnit(),\n                                                                   workQueue,\n                                                                   instrumentedThreadFactory,\n                                                                   handler);\n        executor.allowCoreThreadTimeOut(allowCoreThreadTimeOut);\n        environment.manage(new ExecutorServiceManager(executor, shutdownTime, nameFormat));\n        return executor;\n    }\n\n    static String getNameWithoutFormat(String nameFormat) {\n        final String name = String.format(Locale.ROOT, nameFormat, 0);\n        return commonPrefixWithoutHyphen(name, nameFormat) + commonSuffix(name, nameFormat);\n    }\n\n    static String commonPrefixWithoutHyphen(String name, String nameFormat) {\n        final int minLength = Math.min(name.length(), nameFormat.length());\n        int diffIndex;\n        for (diffIndex = 0; diffIndex < minLength; diffIndex++) {\n            if (name.charAt(diffIndex) != nameFormat.charAt(diffIndex)) {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        if (diffIndex > 0 && name.charAt(diffIndex - 1) == '-') {\n            diffIndex--;\n        }\n        return name.substring(0, diffIndex);\n    }\n\n    static String commonSuffix(String name, String nameFormat) {\n        int nameIndex = name.length();\n        int nameFormatIndex = nameFormat.length();\n        while (--nameIndex >= 0 && --nameFormatIndex >= 0) {\n            if (name.charAt(nameIndex) != nameFormat.charAt(nameFormatIndex)) {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        return name.substring(nameIndex + 1);\n    }\n\n    private boolean isBoundedQueue() {\n        return workQueue.remainingCapacity() != Integer.MAX_VALUE;\n    }\n\n    static synchronized void setLog(Logger newLog) {\n        log = newLog;\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-lifecycle/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/lifecycle/setup/LifecycleEnvironment.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.lifecycle.setup;\n\nimport com.codahale.metrics.MetricRegistry;\nimport io.dropwizard.lifecycle.JettyManaged;\nimport io.dropwizard.lifecycle.Managed;\nimport io.dropwizard.lifecycle.ServerLifecycleListener;\nimport org.eclipse.jetty.server.Server;\nimport org.eclipse.jetty.util.component.AbstractLifeCycle;\nimport org.eclipse.jetty.util.component.ContainerLifeCycle;\nimport org.eclipse.jetty.util.component.LifeCycle;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ThreadFactory;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\npublic class LifecycleEnvironment {\n    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(LifecycleEnvironment.class);\n\n    private final List<LifeCycle> managedObjects;\n    private final List<LifeCycle.Listener> lifecycleListeners;\n    private final MetricRegistry metricRegistry;\n\n    public LifecycleEnvironment(MetricRegistry metricRegistry) {\n        this.managedObjects = new ArrayList<>();\n        this.lifecycleListeners = new ArrayList<>();\n        this.metricRegistry = metricRegistry;\n    }\n\n    public List<LifeCycle> getManagedObjects() {\n        return managedObjects;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds the given {@link Managed} instance to the set of objects managed by the server's\n     * lifecycle. When the server starts, {@code managed} will be started. When the server stops,\n     * {@code managed} will be stopped.\n     *\n     * @param managed a managed object\n     */\n    public void manage(Managed managed) {\n        managedObjects.add(new JettyManaged(requireNonNull(managed)));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds the given Jetty {@link LifeCycle} instances to the server's lifecycle.\n     *\n     * @param managed a Jetty-managed object\n     */\n    public void manage(LifeCycle managed) {\n        managedObjects.add(requireNonNull(managed));\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder executorService(String nameFormat) {\n        return new ExecutorServiceBuilder(this, nameFormat);\n    }\n\n    public ExecutorServiceBuilder executorService(String nameFormat, ThreadFactory factory) {\n        return new ExecutorServiceBuilder(this, nameFormat, factory);\n    }\n\n    public ScheduledExecutorServiceBuilder scheduledExecutorService(String nameFormat) {\n        return scheduledExecutorService(nameFormat, false);\n    }\n\n    public ScheduledExecutorServiceBuilder scheduledExecutorService(String nameFormat, ThreadFactory factory) {\n        return new ScheduledExecutorServiceBuilder(this, nameFormat, factory);\n    }\n\n    public ScheduledExecutorServiceBuilder scheduledExecutorService(String nameFormat, boolean useDaemonThreads) {\n        return new ScheduledExecutorServiceBuilder(this, nameFormat, useDaemonThreads);\n    }\n\n    public void addServerLifecycleListener(ServerLifecycleListener listener) {\n        lifecycleListeners.add(new ServerListener(listener));\n    }\n\n    public void addLifeCycleListener(LifeCycle.Listener listener) {\n        lifecycleListeners.add(listener);\n    }\n\n    public void attach(ContainerLifeCycle container) {\n        for (LifeCycle object : managedObjects) {\n            container.addBean(object);\n        }\n        container.addLifeCycleListener(new AbstractLifeCycle.AbstractLifeCycleListener() {\n            @Override\n            public void lifeCycleStarting(LifeCycle event) {\n                LOGGER.debug(\"managed objects = {}\", managedObjects);\n            }\n        });\n        for (LifeCycle.Listener listener : lifecycleListeners) {\n            container.addLifeCycleListener(listener);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    public MetricRegistry getMetricRegistry() {\n        return metricRegistry;\n    }\n\n    private static class ServerListener extends AbstractLifeCycle.AbstractLifeCycleListener {\n        private final ServerLifecycleListener listener;\n\n        private ServerListener(ServerLifecycleListener listener) {\n            this.listener = listener;\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public void lifeCycleStarted(LifeCycle event) {\n            if (event instanceof Server) {\n                listener.serverStarted((Server) event);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-lifecycle/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/lifecycle/ServerLifecycleListenerTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.lifecycle;\n\nimport org.assertj.core.api.Assertions;\nimport org.eclipse.jetty.server.Connector;\nimport org.eclipse.jetty.server.Server;\nimport org.eclipse.jetty.server.ServerConnector;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Objects;\nimport java.util.stream.Stream;\n\nimport static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;\nimport static org.mockito.Mockito.when;\n\nclass ServerLifecycleListenerTest {\n    private static final String APPLICATION = \"application\";\n    private static final String ADMIN = \"admin\";\n    private static final String HOST_1 = \"192.168.2.5\";\n    private static final String HOST_2 = \"192.68.4.77\";\n    private static final String HOST_3 = \"192.168.67.20\";\n\n    @Test\n    void getLocalPort() {\n        int localPort = 5673;\n        int adminPort = 12345;\n        Server server = configureAndGetSingleConnectorServer(localPort, adminPort);\n        ServerLifecycleListener listener = (server1) -> {\n        };\n        int retrievedLocalPort = listener.getLocalPort(server);\n        Assertions.assertThat(retrievedLocalPort).isEqualTo(localPort);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void getAdminPort() {\n        int localPort = 5673;\n        int adminPort = 12345;\n        Server server = configureAndGetSingleConnectorServer(localPort, adminPort);\n        ServerLifecycleListener listener = (server1) -> {\n        };\n        int retrievedAdminPort = listener.getAdminPort(server);\n        Assertions.assertThat(retrievedAdminPort).isEqualTo(adminPort);\n    }\n\n    private Server configureAndGetSingleConnectorServer(int applicationPort, int adminPort) {\n        Server server = mock(Server.class);\n        ServerConnector applicationConnector = mock(ServerConnector.class);\n        ServerConnector adminConnector = mock(ServerConnector.class);\n        Connector[] connectors = {applicationConnector, adminConnector};\n        when(server.getConnectors()).thenReturn(connectors);\n        configuredServerConnector(applicationConnector, applicationPort, Arrays.asList(\"ssl\", \"http/1.1\", \"http/2\"), APPLICATION, HOST_1);\n        configuredServerConnector(adminConnector, adminPort, Arrays.asList(\"tls\", \"http/2\"), ADMIN, HOST_2);\n        return server;\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void getPortDescriptorList() {\n        Server server = configureMultiProtocolServer();\n        ServerLifecycleListener listener = (server1) -> {\n        };\n        List<PortDescriptor> portDescriptorList = listener.getPortDescriptorList(server);\n        PortDescriptor[] portDescriptors = buildCompletePortDescriptorsArray();\n        Assertions.assertThat(portDescriptorList).usingElementComparator((o1, o2) -> {\n            if (Objects.equals(o1.getConnectorType(), o2.getConnectorType()) &&\n                Objects.equals(o1.getProtocol(), o2.getProtocol()) &&\n                Objects.equals(o1.getPort(), o2.getPort()) &&\n                Objects.equals(o1.getHost(), o2.getHost())) {\n                return 0;\n            } else {\n                return -1;\n            }\n        }).contains(portDescriptors);\n    }\n\n    private PortDescriptor[] buildCompletePortDescriptorsArray() {\n        return Stream.of(getPortDescriptors(5673, ADMIN, new String[]{\"ssl\", \"http/1.1\", \"http/2\"}, HOST_1),\n            getPortDescriptors(12345, APPLICATION, new String[]{\"tls\", \"http/2\"}, HOST_2),\n            getPortDescriptors(1234, APPLICATION, new String[]{\"http/1.1\", \"http/2\", \"websocket\"}, HOST_3))\n            .flatMap(Arrays::stream)\n            .toArray(PortDescriptor[]::new);\n    }\n\n    private PortDescriptor[] getPortDescriptors(int port, String type, String[] protocols, String host) {\n        return Arrays.stream(protocols)\n            .map(protocol -> getPortDescriptor(protocol, port, type, host))\n            .toArray(PortDescriptor[]::new);\n    }\n\n    private PortDescriptor getPortDescriptor(String protocol, int port, String type, String host) {\n        return new PortDescriptor(protocol, port, type, host);\n    }\n\n    private Server configureMultiProtocolServer() {\n        Server server = mock(Server.class);\n        ServerConnector connectorMock1 = mock(ServerConnector.class);\n        ServerConnector connectorMock2 = mock(ServerConnector.class);\n        ServerConnector connectorMock3 = mock(ServerConnector.class);\n        Connector[] connectors = {connectorMock1, connectorMock2, connectorMock3};\n        when(server.getConnectors()).thenReturn(connectors);\n        configuredServerConnector(connectorMock1, 5673, Arrays.asList(\"ssl\", \"http/1.1\", \"http/2\"), ADMIN, HOST_1);\n        configuredServerConnector(connectorMock2, 12345, Arrays.asList(\"tls\", \"http/2\"), APPLICATION, HOST_2);\n        configuredServerConnector(connectorMock3, 1234, Arrays.asList(\"http/1.1\", \"http/2\", \"websocket\"), APPLICATION, \"192.168.67.20\");\n        return server;\n    }\n\n    private void configuredServerConnector(ServerConnector connectorMock1, int localPort, List<String> protocols, String portType, String host) {\n        when(connectorMock1.getLocalPort()).thenReturn(localPort);\n        when(connectorMock1.getProtocols()).thenReturn(protocols);\n        when(connectorMock1.getName()).thenReturn(portType);\n        when(connectorMock1.getHost()).thenReturn(host);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "dropwizard-lifecycle/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/lifecycle/setup/ExecutorServiceBuilderTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.lifecycle.setup;\n\nimport com.codahale.metrics.InstrumentedThreadFactory;\nimport com.codahale.metrics.MetricRegistry;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Duration;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport org.mockito.exceptions.verification.WantedButNotInvoked;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\n\nimport java.util.concurrent.ArrayBlockingQueue;\nimport java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;\nimport java.util.concurrent.Executor;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;\nimport java.util.concurrent.LinkedBlockingQueue;\nimport java.util.concurrent.SynchronousQueue;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ThreadPoolExecutor;\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.mockito.Mockito.anyString;\nimport static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;\nimport static org.mockito.Mockito.never;\nimport static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;\n\nclass ExecutorServiceBuilderTest {\n\n    private static final String WARNING = \"Parameter 'maximumPoolSize' is conflicting with unbounded work queues\";\n\n    private final MetricRegistry metricRegistry = new MetricRegistry();\n    private ExecutorServiceBuilder executorServiceBuilder;\n    private Logger log;\n\n    @BeforeEach\n    void setUp() throws Exception {\n        executorServiceBuilder = new ExecutorServiceBuilder(new LifecycleEnvironment(metricRegistry), \"test-%d\");\n        log = mock(Logger.class);\n        ExecutorServiceBuilder.setLog(log);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void testGiveAWarningAboutMaximumPoolSizeAndUnboundedQueue() {\n        executorServiceBuilder\n            .minThreads(4)\n            .maxThreads(8)\n            .build();\n\n        verify(log).warn(WARNING);\n        assertThat(metricRegistry.getMetrics())\n            .containsOnlyKeys(\"test.created\", \"test.terminated\", \"test.running\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"Slf4jFormatShouldBeConst\")\n    void testGiveNoWarningAboutMaximumPoolSizeAndBoundedQueue() {\n        ExecutorService exe = executorServiceBuilder\n            .minThreads(4)\n            .maxThreads(8)\n            .workQueue(new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(16))\n            .build();\n\n        verify(log, never()).warn(WARNING);\n        assertCanExecuteAtLeast2ConcurrentTasks(exe);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * There should be no warning about using a Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor() equivalent\n     * @see java.util.concurrent.Executors#newSingleThreadExecutor()\n     */\n    @Test\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"Slf4jFormatShouldBeConst\")\n    void shouldNotWarnWhenSettingUpSingleThreadedPool() {\n        executorServiceBuilder\n            .minThreads(1)\n            .maxThreads(1)\n            .keepAliveTime(Duration.milliseconds(0))\n            .workQueue(new LinkedBlockingQueue<>())\n            .build();\n\n        verify(log, never()).warn(anyString());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * There should be no warning about using a Executors.newCachedThreadPool() equivalent\n     * @see java.util.concurrent.Executors#newCachedThreadPool()\n     */\n    @Test\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"Slf4jFormatShouldBeConst\")\n    void shouldNotWarnWhenSettingUpCachedThreadPool() {\n        ExecutorService exe = executorServiceBuilder\n            .minThreads(0)\n            .maxThreads(Integer.MAX_VALUE)\n            .keepAliveTime(Duration.seconds(60))\n            .workQueue(new SynchronousQueue<>())\n            .build();\n\n        verify(log, never()).warn(anyString());\n        assertCanExecuteAtLeast2ConcurrentTasks(exe); // cached thread pools work right?\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"Slf4jFormatShouldBeConst\")\n    void shouldNotWarnWhenUsingTheDefaultConfiguration() {\n        executorServiceBuilder.build();\n        verify(log, never()).warn(anyString());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Setting large max threads without large min threads is misleading on the default queue implementation\n     * It should warn or work\n     */\n    @Test\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"Slf4jFormatShouldBeConst\")\n    void shouldBeAbleToExecute2TasksAtOnceWithLargeMaxThreadsOrBeWarnedOtherwise() {\n        ExecutorService exe = executorServiceBuilder\n            .maxThreads(Integer.MAX_VALUE)\n            .build();\n\n        try {\n            verify(log).warn(anyString());\n        } catch (WantedButNotInvoked error) {\n            // no warning has been given so we should be able to execute at least 2 things at once\n            assertCanExecuteAtLeast2ConcurrentTasks(exe);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void shouldUseInstrumentedThreadFactory() {\n        ExecutorService exe = executorServiceBuilder.build();\n        final ThreadPoolExecutor castedExec = (ThreadPoolExecutor) exe;\n\n        assertThat(castedExec.getThreadFactory()).isInstanceOf(InstrumentedThreadFactory.class);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void nameWithoutFormat() {\n        final String[][] tests = new String[][] {\n            { \"my-client-%d\", \"my-client\" },\n            { \"my-client--%d\", \"my-client-\" },\n            { \"my-client-%d-abc\", \"my-client-abc\" },\n            { \"my-client%d\", \"my-client\" },\n            { \"my-client%d-abc\", \"my-client-abc\" },\n            { \"my-client%s\", \"my-client\" },\n            { \"my-client%sabc\", \"my-clientabc\" },\n            { \"my-client%10d\", \"my-client\" },\n            { \"my-client%10d0\", \"my-client0\" },\n            { \"my-client%-10d\", \"my-client\" },\n            { \"my-client%-10d0\", \"my-client0\" },\n            { \"my-client-%10d\", \"my-client\" },\n            { \"my-client-%10dabc\", \"my-clientabc\" },\n            { \"my-client-%1$d\", \"my-client\" },\n            { \"my-client-%1$d-abc\", \"my-client-abc\" },\n            { \"-%d\", \"\" },\n            { \"%d\", \"\" },\n            { \"%d-abc\", \"-abc\" },\n            { \"%10d\", \"\" },\n            { \"%10dabc\", \"abc\" },\n            { \"%-10d\", \"\" },\n            { \"%-10dabc\", \"abc\" },\n            { \"%10s\", \"\" },\n            { \"%10sabc\", \"abc\" },\n        };\n        for (String[] t : tests) {\n            assertThat(ExecutorServiceBuilder.getNameWithoutFormat(t[0]))\n                .describedAs(\"%s -> %s\", t[0], t[1])\n                .isEqualTo(t[1]);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Tries to run 2 tasks that on the executor that rely on each others side-effect to complete. If they fail to\n     * complete within a short time then we can assume they are not running concurrently\n     * @param exe an executor to try to run 2 tasks on\n     */\n    private void assertCanExecuteAtLeast2ConcurrentTasks(Executor exe) {\n        CountDownLatch latch = new CountDownLatch(2);\n        Runnable concurrentLatchCountDownAndWait = () -> {\n            latch.countDown();\n            try {\n                latch.await();\n            } catch (InterruptedException ex) {\n                throw new RuntimeException(ex);\n            }\n        };\n\n        exe.execute(concurrentLatchCountDownAndWait);\n        exe.execute(concurrentLatchCountDownAndWait);\n\n        try {\n            // 1 second is ages even on a slow VM\n            assertThat(latch.await(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS))\n                .as(\"2 tasks executed concurrently on \" + exe)\n                .isTrue();\n        } catch (InterruptedException ex) {\n            throw new RuntimeException(ex);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "dropwizard-logging/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/logging/AbstractAppenderFactory.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.logging;\n\nimport ch.qos.logback.classic.AsyncAppender;\nimport ch.qos.logback.classic.Level;\nimport ch.qos.logback.classic.LoggerContext;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.Appender;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.AsyncAppenderBase;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.Context;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.LayoutBase;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.pattern.PatternLayoutBase;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.spi.DeferredProcessingAware;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonProperty;\nimport io.dropwizard.logback.ThrottlingAppenderWrapper;\nimport io.dropwizard.logging.async.AsyncAppenderFactory;\nimport io.dropwizard.logging.filter.FilterFactory;\nimport io.dropwizard.logging.layout.DiscoverableLayoutFactory;\nimport io.dropwizard.logging.layout.LayoutFactory;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Duration;\nimport io.dropwizard.validation.MaxDuration;\nimport io.dropwizard.validation.MinDuration;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Nullable;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Max;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Min;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.TimeZone;\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;\n\n/**\n * A base implementation of {@link AppenderFactory}.\n * <p/>\n * <b>Configuration Parameters:</b>\n * <table>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>Name</td>\n *         <td>Default</td>\n *         <td>Description</td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code threshold}</td>\n *         <td>ALL</td>\n *         <td>The minimum event level the appender will handle.</td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code logFormat}</td>\n *         <td>(none)</td>\n *         <td>An appender-specific log format.</td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code timeZone}</td>\n *         <td>{@code UTC}</td>\n *         <td>\n *             The time zone to which event timestamps will be converted.\n *             Ignored if logFormat is supplied.\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code queueSize}</td>\n *         <td>{@link AsyncAppenderBase}</td>\n *         <td>The maximum capacity of the blocking queue.</td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code includeCallerData}</td>\n *         <td>{@link AsyncAppenderBase}</td>\n *         <td>\n *             Whether to include caller data, required for line numbers.\n *             Beware, is considered expensive.\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code discardingThreshold}</td>\n *         <td>{@link AsyncAppenderBase}</td>\n *         <td>\n *             By default, when the blocking queue has 20% capacity remaining,\n *             it will drop events of level TRACE, DEBUG and INFO, keeping only\n *             events of level WARN and ERROR. To keep all events, set discardingThreshold to 0.\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code messageRate}</td>\n *         <td>\n *             Maximum message rate: average duration between messages. Extra messages are discarded.\n *             This setting avoids flooding a paid logging service by accident.\n *             For example, a duration of 100ms allows for a maximum of 10 messages per second and 30s would mean\n *             1 message every 30 seconds.\n *             The maximum acceptable duration is 1 minute.\n *             By default, this duration is not set and this feature is disabled.\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code filterFactories}</td>\n *         <td>(none)</td>\n *         <td>\n *             A list of {@link FilterFactory filters} to apply to the appender, in order,\n *             after the {@code threshold}.\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n * </table>\n */\npublic abstract class AbstractAppenderFactory<E extends DeferredProcessingAware> implements AppenderFactory<E> {\n    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(AbstractAppenderFactory.class);\n\n    @NotNull\n    protected Level threshold = Level.ALL;\n\n    @Nullable\n    protected String logFormat;\n\n    @Nullable\n    protected DiscoverableLayoutFactory<E> layout;\n\n    @NotNull\n    protected TimeZone timeZone = TimeZone.getTimeZone(\"UTC\");\n\n    @Min(1)\n    @Max(Integer.MAX_VALUE)\n    private int queueSize = AsyncAppenderBase.DEFAULT_QUEUE_SIZE;\n\n    private int discardingThreshold = -1;\n\n    @Nullable\n    @MinDuration(value = 0, unit = TimeUnit.SECONDS, inclusive = false)\n    @MaxDuration(value = 1, unit = TimeUnit.MINUTES)\n    private Duration messageRate;\n\n    private boolean includeCallerData = false;\n\n    private List<FilterFactory<E>> filterFactories = Collections.emptyList();\n\n    private boolean neverBlock = false;\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public int getQueueSize() {\n        return queueSize;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setQueueSize(int queueSize) {\n        this.queueSize = queueSize;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public int getDiscardingThreshold() {\n        return discardingThreshold;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setDiscardingThreshold(int discardingThreshold) {\n        this.discardingThreshold = discardingThreshold;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    @JsonProperty\n    @Nullable\n    public Duration getMessageRate() {\n        return messageRate;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setMessageRate(Duration messageRate) {\n        this.messageRate = messageRate;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public String getThreshold() {\n        return threshold.toString();\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setThreshold(String threshold) {\n        this.threshold = DefaultLoggingFactory.toLevel(threshold);\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    @Nullable\n    public String getLogFormat() {\n        return logFormat;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setLogFormat(@Nullable String logFormat) {\n        this.logFormat = logFormat;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public TimeZone getTimeZone() {\n        return timeZone;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setTimeZone(String zoneId) {\n        this.timeZone = \"system\".equalsIgnoreCase(zoneId) ? TimeZone.getDefault() :\n            TimeZone.getTimeZone(zoneId);\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setTimeZone(TimeZone timeZone) {\n        this.timeZone = timeZone;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public boolean isIncludeCallerData() {\n        return includeCallerData;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setIncludeCallerData(boolean includeCallerData) {\n        this.includeCallerData = includeCallerData;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public List<FilterFactory<E>> getFilterFactories() {\n        return filterFactories;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setFilterFactories(List<FilterFactory<E>> appenders) {\n        this.filterFactories = new ArrayList<>(appenders);\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setNeverBlock(boolean neverBlock) {\n        this.neverBlock = neverBlock;\n    }\n\n    @Nullable\n    @JsonProperty\n    public DiscoverableLayoutFactory<?> getLayout() {\n        return layout;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setLayout(@Nullable DiscoverableLayoutFactory<E> layout) {\n        this.layout = layout;\n    }\n\n    protected Appender<E> wrapAsync(Appender<E> appender, AsyncAppenderFactory<E> asyncAppenderFactory) {\n        return wrapAsync(appender, asyncAppenderFactory, appender.getContext());\n    }\n\n    protected Appender<E> wrapAsync(Appender<E> appender, AsyncAppenderFactory<E> asyncAppenderFactory, Context context) {\n        final AsyncAppenderBase<E> asyncAppender = asyncAppenderFactory.build();\n        if (asyncAppender instanceof AsyncAppender) {\n            ((AsyncAppender) asyncAppender).setIncludeCallerData(includeCallerData);\n        }\n        asyncAppender.setQueueSize(queueSize);\n        asyncAppender.setDiscardingThreshold(discardingThreshold);\n        asyncAppender.setContext(context);\n        asyncAppender.setName(\"async-\" + appender.getName());\n        asyncAppender.addAppender(appender);\n        asyncAppender.setNeverBlock(neverBlock);\n        asyncAppender.start();\n        if (messageRate == null) {\n            return asyncAppender;\n        } else {\n            return new ThrottlingAppenderWrapper<>(asyncAppender, messageRate.getQuantity(), messageRate.getUnit());\n        }\n    }\n\n    protected LayoutBase<E> buildLayout(LoggerContext context, LayoutFactory<E> defaultLayoutFactory) {\n        final LayoutBase<E> layoutBase;\n        if (layout == null) {\n            layoutBase = defaultLayoutFactory.build(context, timeZone);\n        } else {\n            layoutBase = layout.build(context, timeZone);\n        }\n        if (!(logFormat == null || logFormat.isEmpty())) {\n            if (layoutBase instanceof PatternLayoutBase) {\n                @SuppressWarnings(\"NullAway\")\n                String logFormatWithTimeZone = logFormat.replace(\"%dwTimeZone\", timeZone.getID());\n                ((PatternLayoutBase<E>)layoutBase).setPattern(logFormatWithTimeZone);\n            } else {\n                LOGGER.warn(\"Ignoring 'logFormat', because 'layout' does not extend PatternLayoutBase\");\n            }\n        }\n\n        layoutBase.start();\n        return layoutBase;\n    }\n}\n"
