      "content": "package io.dropwizard.logging;\n\nimport ch.qos.logback.classic.Level;\nimport ch.qos.logback.classic.Logger;\nimport ch.qos.logback.classic.LoggerContext;\nimport ch.qos.logback.classic.jmx.JMXConfigurator;\nimport ch.qos.logback.classic.jul.LevelChangePropagator;\nimport ch.qos.logback.classic.spi.ILoggingEvent;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.Appender;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.AsyncAppenderBase;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.ConsoleAppender;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.encoder.LayoutWrappingEncoder;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.util.StatusPrinter;\nimport com.codahale.metrics.MetricRegistry;\nimport com.codahale.metrics.logback.InstrumentedAppender;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnore;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonProperty;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonTypeName;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;\nimport io.dropwizard.jackson.Jackson;\nimport io.dropwizard.logback.AsyncAppenderBaseProxy;\nimport io.dropwizard.logging.async.AsyncAppenderFactory;\nimport io.dropwizard.logging.async.AsyncLoggingEventAppenderFactory;\nimport io.dropwizard.logging.filter.LevelFilterFactory;\nimport io.dropwizard.logging.filter.ThresholdLevelFilterFactory;\nimport io.dropwizard.logging.layout.DropwizardLayoutFactory;\nimport io.dropwizard.logging.layout.LayoutFactory;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Nullable;\nimport javax.management.InstanceAlreadyExistsException;\nimport javax.management.MBeanRegistrationException;\nimport javax.management.MBeanServer;\nimport javax.management.MalformedObjectNameException;\nimport javax.management.NotCompliantMBeanException;\nimport javax.management.ObjectName;\nimport javax.validation.Valid;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;\nimport java.io.PrintStream;\nimport java.lang.management.ManagementFactory;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.HashMap;\nimport java.util.Iterator;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport java.util.TimeZone;\nimport java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\n@JsonTypeName(\"default\")\npublic class DefaultLoggingFactory implements LoggingFactory {\n    private static final ReentrantLock MBEAN_REGISTRATION_LOCK = new ReentrantLock();\n    private static final ReentrantLock CHANGE_LOGGER_CONTEXT_LOCK = new ReentrantLock();\n\n    @NotNull\n    private String level = \"INFO\";\n\n    @NotNull\n    private Map<String, JsonNode> loggers = Collections.emptyMap();\n\n    @Valid\n    @NotNull\n    private List<AppenderFactory<ILoggingEvent>> appenders = Collections.singletonList(new ConsoleAppenderFactory<>());\n\n    @JsonIgnore\n    private final LoggerContext loggerContext;\n\n    @JsonIgnore\n    private final PrintStream configurationErrorsStream;\n\n    public DefaultLoggingFactory() {\n        this(LoggingUtil.getLoggerContext(), System.err);\n    }\n\n    DefaultLoggingFactory(LoggerContext loggerContext, PrintStream configurationErrorsStream) {\n        super();\n        this.loggerContext = requireNonNull(loggerContext);\n        this.configurationErrorsStream = requireNonNull(configurationErrorsStream);\n    }\n\n    LoggerContext getLoggerContext() {\n        return loggerContext;\n    }\n\n    PrintStream getConfigurationErrorsStream() {\n        return configurationErrorsStream;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public String getLevel() {\n        return level;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setLevel(String level) {\n        this.level = level;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public Map<String, JsonNode> getLoggers() {\n        return loggers;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setLoggers(Map<String, JsonNode> loggers) {\n        this.loggers = new HashMap<>(loggers);\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public List<AppenderFactory<ILoggingEvent>> getAppenders() {\n        return appenders;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setAppenders(List<AppenderFactory<ILoggingEvent>> appenders) {\n        this.appenders = new ArrayList<>(appenders);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void configure(MetricRegistry metricRegistry, String name) {\n        LoggingUtil.hijackJDKLogging();\n\n        CHANGE_LOGGER_CONTEXT_LOCK.lock();\n        final Logger root;\n        try {\n            root = configureLoggers(name);\n        } finally {\n            CHANGE_LOGGER_CONTEXT_LOCK.unlock();\n        }\n\n        final LevelFilterFactory<ILoggingEvent> levelFilterFactory = new ThresholdLevelFilterFactory();\n        final AsyncAppenderFactory<ILoggingEvent> asyncAppenderFactory = new AsyncLoggingEventAppenderFactory();\n        final LayoutFactory<ILoggingEvent> layoutFactory = new DropwizardLayoutFactory();\n\n        for (AppenderFactory<ILoggingEvent> output : appenders) {\n            root.addAppender(output.build(loggerContext, name, layoutFactory, levelFilterFactory, asyncAppenderFactory));\n        }\n\n        StatusPrinter.setPrintStream(configurationErrorsStream);\n        try {\n            StatusPrinter.printIfErrorsOccured(loggerContext);\n        } finally {\n            StatusPrinter.setPrintStream(System.out);\n        }\n\n        final MBeanServer server = ManagementFactory.getPlatformMBeanServer();\n        MBEAN_REGISTRATION_LOCK.lock();\n        try {\n            final ObjectName objectName = new ObjectName(\"io.dropwizard:type=Logging\");\n            if (!server.isRegistered(objectName)) {\n                server.registerMBean(new JMXConfigurator(loggerContext,\n                                server,\n                                objectName),\n                        objectName);\n            }\n        } catch (MalformedObjectNameException | InstanceAlreadyExistsException |\n                NotCompliantMBeanException | MBeanRegistrationException e) {\n            throw new RuntimeException(e);\n        } finally {\n            MBEAN_REGISTRATION_LOCK.unlock();\n        }\n\n        configureInstrumentation(root, metricRegistry);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void stop() {\n        // Should acquire the lock to avoid concurrent listener changes\n        CHANGE_LOGGER_CONTEXT_LOCK.lock();\n        try {\n            // We need to go through a list of appenders and locate the async ones,\n            // as those could have messages left to write. Since there is no flushing\n            // mechanism built into logback, we wait for a short period of time before\n            // giving up that the appender will be completely flushed.\n            final Logger logger = loggerContext.getLogger(org.slf4j.Logger.ROOT_LOGGER_NAME);\n            final Iterator<Appender<ILoggingEvent>> appenders = logger.iteratorForAppenders();\n            while (appenders.hasNext()) {\n                final Appender<ILoggingEvent> appender = appenders.next();\n                if (appender instanceof AsyncAppenderBase) {\n                    flushAppender((AsyncAppenderBase<?>) appender);\n                } else if (appender instanceof AsyncAppenderBaseProxy) {\n                    flushAppender(((AsyncAppenderBaseProxy<?>) appender).getAppender());\n                }\n            }\n        } catch (InterruptedException ignored) {\n            // If the thread waiting for the logs to be flushed is aborted then\n            // user clearly wants the application to quit now, so stop trying\n            // to flush any appenders\n            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();\n        } finally {\n            CHANGE_LOGGER_CONTEXT_LOCK.unlock();\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void reset() {\n        CHANGE_LOGGER_CONTEXT_LOCK.lock();\n        try {\n            // Flush all the loggers and reinstate only the console logger as a\n            // sane default.\n            loggerContext.stop();\n            final Logger logger = loggerContext.getLogger(org.slf4j.Logger.ROOT_LOGGER_NAME);\n            logger.detachAndStopAllAppenders();\n            final DropwizardLayout formatter = new DropwizardLayout(loggerContext, TimeZone.getDefault());\n            formatter.start();\n            final LayoutWrappingEncoder<ILoggingEvent> layoutEncoder = new LayoutWrappingEncoder<>();\n            layoutEncoder.setLayout(formatter);\n            final ConsoleAppender<ILoggingEvent> consoleAppender = new ConsoleAppender<>();\n            consoleAppender.addFilter(new ThresholdLevelFilterFactory().build(Level.INFO));\n            consoleAppender.setEncoder(layoutEncoder);\n            consoleAppender.setContext(loggerContext);\n            consoleAppender.start();\n            logger.addAppender(consoleAppender);\n            loggerContext.start();\n        } finally {\n            CHANGE_LOGGER_CONTEXT_LOCK.unlock();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void flushAppender(AsyncAppenderBase<?> appender) throws InterruptedException {\n        int timeWaiting = 0;\n        while (timeWaiting < appender.getMaxFlushTime() && appender.getNumberOfElementsInQueue() > 0) {\n            Thread.sleep(100);\n            timeWaiting += 100;\n        }\n\n        if (appender.getNumberOfElementsInQueue() > 0) {\n            // It may seem odd to log when we're trying to flush a logger that\n            // isn't flushing, but the same warning is issued inside\n            // appender.stop() if the appender isn't able to flush.\n            appender.addWarn(appender.getNumberOfElementsInQueue() + \" events may be discarded\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void configureInstrumentation(Logger root, MetricRegistry metricRegistry) {\n        final InstrumentedAppender appender = new InstrumentedAppender(metricRegistry);\n        appender.setContext(loggerContext);\n        appender.start();\n        root.addAppender(appender);\n    }\n\n    private Logger configureLoggers(String name) {\n        final Logger root = loggerContext.getLogger(org.slf4j.Logger.ROOT_LOGGER_NAME);\n        loggerContext.reset();\n\n        final LevelChangePropagator propagator = new LevelChangePropagator();\n        propagator.setContext(loggerContext);\n        propagator.setResetJUL(true);\n\n        loggerContext.addListener(propagator);\n\n        root.setLevel(toLevel(level));\n\n        final LevelFilterFactory<ILoggingEvent> levelFilterFactory = new ThresholdLevelFilterFactory();\n        final AsyncAppenderFactory<ILoggingEvent> asyncAppenderFactory = new AsyncLoggingEventAppenderFactory();\n        final LayoutFactory<ILoggingEvent> layoutFactory = new DropwizardLayoutFactory();\n\n        for (Map.Entry<String, JsonNode> entry : loggers.entrySet()) {\n            final Logger logger = loggerContext.getLogger(entry.getKey());\n            final JsonNode jsonNode = entry.getValue();\n            if (jsonNode.isTextual() || jsonNode.isBoolean()) {\n                // Just a level as a string\n                logger.setLevel(toLevel(jsonNode.asText()));\n            } else if (jsonNode.isObject()) {\n                // A level and an appender\n                final LoggerConfiguration configuration;\n                try {\n                    configuration = Jackson.newObjectMapper().treeToValue(jsonNode, LoggerConfiguration.class);\n                } catch (JsonProcessingException e) {\n                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Wrong format of logger '\" + entry.getKey() + \"'\", e);\n                }\n                logger.setLevel(toLevel(configuration.getLevel()));\n                logger.setAdditive(configuration.isAdditive());\n\n                for (AppenderFactory<ILoggingEvent> appender : configuration.getAppenders()) {\n                    logger.addAppender(appender.build(loggerContext, name, layoutFactory, levelFilterFactory, asyncAppenderFactory));\n                }\n            } else {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Unsupported format of logger '\" + entry.getKey() + \"'\");\n            }\n        }\n\n        return root;\n    }\n\n    static Level toLevel(@Nullable String text) {\n        if (\"false\".equalsIgnoreCase(text)) {\n            // required because YAML maps \"off\" to a boolean false\n            return Level.OFF;\n        } else if (\"true\".equalsIgnoreCase(text)) {\n            // required because YAML maps \"on\" to a boolean true\n            return Level.ALL;\n        }\n        return Level.toLevel(text, Level.INFO);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n        return \"DefaultLoggingFactory{\"\n                + \"level=\" + level\n                + \", loggers=\" + loggers\n                + \", appenders=\" + appenders\n                + '}';\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-logging/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/logging/FileAppenderFactory.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.logging;\n\nimport ch.qos.logback.classic.LoggerContext;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.FileAppender;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.OutputStreamAppender;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.rolling.DefaultTimeBasedFileNamingAndTriggeringPolicy;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.rolling.FixedWindowRollingPolicy;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.rolling.RollingFileAppender;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.rolling.SizeAndTimeBasedRollingPolicy;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.rolling.SizeBasedTriggeringPolicy;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.rolling.TimeBasedFileNamingAndTriggeringPolicy;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.rolling.TimeBasedRollingPolicy;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.spi.DeferredProcessingAware;\nimport ch.qos.logback.core.util.FileSize;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnore;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonProperty;\nimport com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonTypeName;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.DataSize;\nimport io.dropwizard.validation.MinDataSize;\nimport io.dropwizard.validation.ValidationMethod;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Nullable;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Min;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\n/**\n * An {@link AppenderFactory} implementation which provides an appender that writes events to a file, archiving older\n * files as it goes.\n * <p/>\n * <b>Configuration Parameters:</b>\n * <table>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>Name</td>\n *         <td>Default</td>\n *         <td>Description</td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code type}</td>\n *         <td><b>REQUIRED</b></td>\n *         <td>The appender type. Must be {@code file}.</td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code threshold}</td>\n *         <td>{@code ALL}</td>\n *         <td>The lowest level of events to write to the file.</td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code currentLogFilename}</td>\n *         <td><b>REQUIRED</b></td>\n *         <td>The filename where current events are logged.</td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code archive}</td>\n *         <td>{@code true}</td>\n *         <td>Whether or not to archive old events in separate files.</td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code archivedLogFilenamePattern}</td>\n *         <td><b>REQUIRED</b> if {@code archive} is {@code true}.</td>\n *         <td>\n *             The filename pattern for archived files.\n *             If {@code maxFileSize} is specified, rollover is size-based, and the pattern must contain {@code %i} for\n *             an integer index of the archived file.\n *             Otherwise rollover is date-based, and the pattern must contain {@code %d}, which is replaced with the\n *             date in {@code yyyy-MM-dd} form.\n *             If the pattern ends with {@code .gz} or {@code .zip}, files will be compressed as they are archived.\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code archivedFileCount}</td>\n *         <td>{@code 5}</td>\n *         <td>\n *             The number of archived files to keep. Must be greater than or equal to {@code 0}. Zero is a\n *             special value signifying to keep infinite logs (use with caution)\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code maxFileSize}</td>\n *         <td>(unlimited)</td>\n *         <td>\n *             The maximum size of the currently active file before a rollover is triggered. The value can be expressed\n *             with SI and IEC prefixes, see {@link io.dropwizard.util.DataSizeUnit}.\n *             Examples include 100MiB, 1GiB, 1TiB.  Sizes can also be spelled out, such as 100 mebibytes,\n *             1 gibibyte, 1 tebibyte.\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code totalSizeCap}</td>\n *         <td>(unlimited)</td>\n *         <td>\n *             Controls the total size of all files. Oldest archives are deleted asynchronously when the total\n *             size cap is exceeded.\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code timeZone}</td>\n *         <td>{@code UTC}</td>\n *         <td>The time zone to which event timestamps will be converted.</td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code logFormat}</td>\n *         <td>the default format</td>\n *         <td>\n *             The Logback pattern with which events will be formatted. See\n *             <a href=\"http://logback.qos.ch/manual/layouts.html#conversionWord\">the Logback documentation</a>\n *             for details.\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>{@code bufferSize}</td>\n *         <td>8KiB</td>\n *         <td>\n *             The buffer size of the underlying FileAppender (setting added in logback 1.1.10). Increasing this from\n *             the default of 8KiB to 256KiB is reported to significantly reduce thread contention.\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n *      <tr>\n *         <td>{@code immediateFlush}</td>\n *         <td>{@code true}</td>\n *         <td>\n *             If set to true, log events will be immediately flushed to disk. Immediate flushing is safer, but\n *             it degrades logging throughput.\n *             See <a href=\"https://logback.qos.ch/manual/appenders.html#immediateFlush\">the Logback documentation</a>\n *             for details.\n *         </td>\n *     </tr>\n * </table>\n *\n * @see AbstractAppenderFactory\n */\n@JsonTypeName(\"file\")\npublic class FileAppenderFactory<E extends DeferredProcessingAware> extends AbstractOutputStreamAppenderFactory<E> {\n\n    @Nullable\n    private String currentLogFilename;\n\n    private boolean archive = true;\n\n    @Nullable\n    private String archivedLogFilenamePattern;\n\n    @Min(0)\n    private int archivedFileCount = 5;\n\n    @Nullable\n    private DataSize maxFileSize;\n\n    @Nullable\n    private DataSize totalSizeCap;\n\n    @MinDataSize(1)\n    private DataSize bufferSize = DataSize.bytes(FileAppender.DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE);\n\n    private boolean immediateFlush = true;\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    @Nullable\n    public String getCurrentLogFilename() {\n        return currentLogFilename;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setCurrentLogFilename(@Nullable String currentLogFilename) {\n        this.currentLogFilename = currentLogFilename;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public boolean isArchive() {\n        return archive;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setArchive(boolean archive) {\n        this.archive = archive;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    @Nullable\n    public String getArchivedLogFilenamePattern() {\n        return archivedLogFilenamePattern;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setArchivedLogFilenamePattern(String archivedLogFilenamePattern) {\n        this.archivedLogFilenamePattern = archivedLogFilenamePattern;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public int getArchivedFileCount() {\n        return archivedFileCount;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setArchivedFileCount(int archivedFileCount) {\n        this.archivedFileCount = archivedFileCount;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    @Nullable\n    public DataSize getMaxFileSize() {\n        return maxFileSize;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setMaxFileSize(@Nullable DataSize maxFileSize) {\n        this.maxFileSize = maxFileSize;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    @JsonProperty\n    @Nullable\n    public DataSize getTotalSizeCap() {\n        return totalSizeCap;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setTotalSizeCap(@Nullable DataSize totalSizeCap) {\n        this.totalSizeCap = totalSizeCap;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public DataSize getBufferSize() {\n        return bufferSize;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setBufferSize(DataSize bufferSize) {\n        this.bufferSize = bufferSize;\n    }\n\n    public boolean isImmediateFlush() {\n        return immediateFlush;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setImmediateFlush(boolean immediateFlush) {\n        this.immediateFlush = immediateFlush;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    @JsonIgnore\n    @ValidationMethod(message = \"totalSizeCap has no effect when using maxFileSize and an archivedLogFilenamePattern without %d, as archivedFileCount implicitly controls the total size cap\")\n    public boolean isTotalSizeCapValid() {\n        return !archive || totalSizeCap == null ||\n            !(maxFileSize != null && !requireNonNull(archivedLogFilenamePattern).contains(\"%d\"));\n    }\n\n    @JsonIgnore\n    @ValidationMethod(message = \"must have archivedLogFilenamePattern if archive is true\")\n    public boolean isValidArchiveConfiguration() {\n        return !archive || (archivedLogFilenamePattern != null);\n    }\n\n    @JsonIgnore\n    @ValidationMethod(message = \"when specifying maxFileSize, archivedLogFilenamePattern must contain %i\")\n    public boolean isValidForMaxFileSizeSetting() {\n        return !archive || maxFileSize == null ||\n                (archivedLogFilenamePattern != null && archivedLogFilenamePattern.contains(\"%i\"));\n    }\n\n    @JsonIgnore\n    @ValidationMethod(message = \"when archivedLogFilenamePattern contains %i, maxFileSize must be specified\")\n    public boolean isMaxFileSizeSettingSpecified() {\n        return !archive || !(archivedLogFilenamePattern != null && archivedLogFilenamePattern.contains(\"%i\")) ||\n                maxFileSize != null;\n    }\n\n    @JsonIgnore\n    @ValidationMethod(message = \"currentLogFilename can only be null when archiving is enabled\")\n    public boolean isValidFileConfiguration() {\n        return archive || currentLogFilename != null;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    protected OutputStreamAppender<E> appender(LoggerContext context) {\n        final FileAppender<E> appender = buildAppender(context);\n        appender.setName(\"file-appender\");\n        appender.setAppend(true);\n        appender.setContext(context);\n        appender.setImmediateFlush(immediateFlush);\n        appender.setPrudent(false);\n        return appender;\n    }\n\n    protected FileAppender<E> buildAppender(LoggerContext context) {\n        if (archive) {\n            final RollingFileAppender<E> appender = new RollingFileAppender<>();\n            appender.setContext(context);\n            appender.setFile(currentLogFilename);\n            appender.setBufferSize(new FileSize(bufferSize.toBytes()));\n\n            if (maxFileSize != null && !requireNonNull(archivedLogFilenamePattern).contains(\"%d\")) {\n                final FixedWindowRollingPolicy rollingPolicy = new FixedWindowRollingPolicy();\n                rollingPolicy.setContext(context);\n                rollingPolicy.setMaxIndex(getArchivedFileCount());\n                rollingPolicy.setFileNamePattern(getArchivedLogFilenamePattern());\n                rollingPolicy.setParent(appender);\n                rollingPolicy.start();\n                appender.setRollingPolicy(rollingPolicy);\n\n                final SizeBasedTriggeringPolicy<E> triggeringPolicy = new SizeBasedTriggeringPolicy<>();\n                triggeringPolicy.setMaxFileSize(new FileSize(maxFileSize.toBytes()));\n                triggeringPolicy.setContext(context);\n                triggeringPolicy.start();\n                appender.setTriggeringPolicy(triggeringPolicy);\n\n                return appender;\n            } else {\n                final TimeBasedRollingPolicy<E> rollingPolicy;\n                if (maxFileSize == null) {\n                    rollingPolicy = new TimeBasedRollingPolicy<>();\n\n                    final TimeBasedFileNamingAndTriggeringPolicy<E> triggeringPolicy = new DefaultTimeBasedFileNamingAndTriggeringPolicy<>();\n                    triggeringPolicy.setContext(context);\n                    triggeringPolicy.setTimeBasedRollingPolicy(rollingPolicy);\n                    appender.setTriggeringPolicy(triggeringPolicy);\n                } else {\n                    // Creating a size and time policy does not need a separate triggering policy set\n                    // on the appender because this policy registers the trigger policy\n                    final SizeAndTimeBasedRollingPolicy<E> sizeAndTimeBasedRollingPolicy = new SizeAndTimeBasedRollingPolicy<>();\n                    sizeAndTimeBasedRollingPolicy.setMaxFileSize(new FileSize(maxFileSize.toBytes()));\n                    rollingPolicy = sizeAndTimeBasedRollingPolicy;\n                }\n\n                if (totalSizeCap != null) {\n                    rollingPolicy.setTotalSizeCap(new FileSize(totalSizeCap.toBytes()));\n                }\n\n                rollingPolicy.setContext(context);\n                rollingPolicy.setFileNamePattern(archivedLogFilenamePattern);\n                rollingPolicy.setMaxHistory(archivedFileCount);\n\n                appender.setRollingPolicy(rollingPolicy);\n\n                rollingPolicy.setParent(appender);\n                rollingPolicy.start();\n                return appender;\n            }\n        }\n\n        final FileAppender<E> appender = new FileAppender<>();\n        appender.setContext(context);\n        appender.setFile(currentLogFilename);\n        appender.setBufferSize(new FileSize(bufferSize.toBytes()));\n        return appender;\n    }\n}\n"

metrics.</td>\n *     </tr>\n *     <tr>\n *         <td>reportOnStop</td>\n *         <td>{@code false}</td>\n *         <td>To report metrics one last time when stopping Dropwizard.</td>\n *     </tr>\n * </table>\n */\npublic class MetricsFactory {\n    private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(MetricsFactory.class);\n\n    @Valid\n    @NotNull\n    private Duration frequency = Duration.minutes(1);\n\n    @Valid\n    @NotNull\n    private List<ReporterFactory> reporters = Collections.emptyList();\n\n    private boolean reportOnStop = false;\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public List<ReporterFactory> getReporters() {\n        return reporters;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setReporters(List<ReporterFactory> reporters) {\n        this.reporters = new ArrayList<>(reporters);\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public Duration getFrequency() {\n        return frequency;\n    }\n\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setFrequency(Duration frequency) {\n        this.frequency = frequency;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    @JsonProperty\n    public boolean isReportOnStop() {\n        return reportOnStop;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    @JsonProperty\n    public void setReportOnStop(boolean reportOnStop) {\n        this.reportOnStop = reportOnStop;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    @JsonProperty\n\n\n    /**\n     * Configures the given lifecycle with the {@link com.codahale.metrics.ScheduledReporter\n     * reporters} configured for the given registry.\n     * <p />\n     * The reporters are tied in to the given lifecycle, such that their {@link #getFrequency()\n     * frequency} for reporting metrics begins when the lifecycle {@link\n     * io.dropwizard.lifecycle.Managed#start() starts}, and stops when the lifecycle\n     * {@link io.dropwizard.lifecycle.Managed#stop() stops}.\n     *\n     * @param environment the lifecycle to manage the reporters.\n     * @param registry the metric registry to report metrics from.\n     */\n    public void configure(LifecycleEnvironment environment, MetricRegistry registry) {\n        for (ReporterFactory reporter : reporters) {\n            try {\n                final ScheduledReporterManager manager =\n                        new ScheduledReporterManager(reporter.build(registry),\n environment.manage(manager);\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                LOGGER.warn(\"Failed to create reporter, metrics may not be properly reported.\", e);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n        return \"MetricsFactory{frequency=\" + frequency + \", reporters=\" + reporters + \", reportOnStop=\" + reportOnStop + '}';\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.metrics;\n\nimport com.codahale.metrics.ScheduledReporter;\nimport io.dropwizard.lifecycle.Managed;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Duration;\n\n/**\n * Manages a {@link ScheduledReporter} lifecycle.\n */\npublic class ScheduledReporterManager implements Managed {\n    private final ScheduledReporter reporter;\n    private final Duration period;\n    private final boolean reportOnStop;\n\n    /**\n     * Manages the given {@code reporter} by reporting with the given {@code period}.\n     *\n     * @param reporter the reporter to manage.\n     * @param period   the frequency to report metrics at.\n     * @see #ScheduledReporterManager(ScheduledReporter, Duration, boolean)\n     */\n    public ScheduledReporterManager(ScheduledReporter reporter, Duration period) {\n        this(reporter, period, false);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Manages the given {@code reporter} by reporting with the given {@code period}.\n     *\n     * @param reporter     the reporter to manage.\n     * @param period       the frequency to report metrics at.\n     * @param reportOnStop whether the reporter should send one last report upon stopping\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    public ScheduledReporterManager(ScheduledReporter reporter, Duration period, boolean reportOnStop) {\n        this.reporter = reporter;\n        this.period = period;\n        this.reportOnStop = reportOnStop;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Begins reporting metrics using the configured {@link ScheduledReporter}.\n     *\n     * @throws Exception\n     */\n    @Override\n    public void start() throws Exception {\n        reporter.start(period.getQuantity(), period.getUnit());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Stops the configured {@link ScheduledReporter} from reporting metrics.\n     *\n     * @throws Exception\n     */\n    @Override\n    public void stop() throws Exception {\n        try {\n            if (reportOnStop) {\n                reporter.report();\n            }\n        } finally {\n            reporter.stop();\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.util;\n\nimport java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.InputStream;\nimport java.io.OutputStream;\n\n/**\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic final class ByteStreams {\n\n    private ByteStreams() {\n    }\n\n    public static byte[] toByteArray(InputStream in) throws IOException {\n        ByteArrayOutputStream to = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n        copyInternal(in, to);\n        return to.toByteArray();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @deprecated this is an internal method to dropwizard-util. Consider apache-commons instead.\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public static void copy(InputStream in, OutputStream to) throws IOException {\n        copyInternal(in, to);\n    }\n\n    static void copyInternal(InputStream in, OutputStream to) throws IOException {\n        byte[] buffer = new byte[4096];\n        int length;\n        while ((length = in.read(buffer)) != -1) {\n            to.write(buffer, 0, length);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

      "path": "dropwizard-util/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/util/DirectExecutorService.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.util;\n\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.concurrent.AbstractExecutorService;\nimport java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionException;\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;\n\n/**\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic class DirectExecutorService extends AbstractExecutorService {\n    /**\n     * Lock used whenever accessing the state variables (runningTasks, shutdown) of the executor\n     */\n    private final Object lock = new Object();\n\n    /*\n     * Conceptually, these two variables describe the executor being in\n     * one of three states:\n     *   - Active: shutdown == false\n     *   - Shutdown: runningTasks > 0 and shutdown == true\n     *   - Terminated: runningTasks == 0 and shutdown == true\n     */\n    private int runningTasks = 0;\n\n    private boolean shutdown = false;\n\n    @Override\n    public void execute(Runnable command) {\n        startTask();\n        try {\n            command.run();\n        } finally {\n            endTask();\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isShutdown() {\n        synchronized (lock) {\n            return shutdown;\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void shutdown() {\n        synchronized (lock) {\n            shutdown = true;\n            if (runningTasks == 0) {\n                lock.notifyAll();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // See newDirectExecutorService javadoc for unusual behavior of this method.\n    @Override\n    public List<Runnable> shutdownNow() {\n        shutdown();\n        return Collections.emptyList();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isTerminated() {\n        synchronized (lock) {\n            return shutdown && runningTasks == 0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean awaitTermination(long timeout, TimeUnit unit) throws InterruptedException {\n        long nanos = unit.toNanos(timeout);\n        synchronized (lock) {\n            while (true) {\n                if (shutdown && runningTasks == 0) {\n                    return true;\n                } else if (nanos <= 0) {\n                    return false;\n                } else {\n                    long now = System.nanoTime();\n                    TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.timedWait(lock, nanos);\n                    nanos -= System.nanoTime() - now; // subtract the actual time we waited\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if the executor has been shut down and increments the running task count.\n     *\n     * @throws RejectedExecutionException if the executor has been previously shutdown\n     */\n    private void startTask() {\n        synchronized (lock) {\n            if (shutdown) {\n                throw new RejectedExecutionException(\"Executor already shutdown\");\n            }\n            runningTasks++;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Decrements the running task count.\n     */\n    private void endTask() {\n        synchronized (lock) {\n            int numRunning = --runningTasks;\n            if (numRunning == 0) {\n                lock.notifyAll();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.util;\n\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.InputStream;\nimport java.io.OutputStream;\nimport java.net.URL;\nimport java.nio.charset.Charset;\n\n/**\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic final class Resources {\n    private Resources() {\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Returns a {@code URL} pointing to {@code resourceName} if the resource is found using the\n     * {@linkplain Thread#getContextClassLoader() context class loader}. In simple environments, the\n     * context class loader will find resources from the class path. In environments where different\n     * threads can have different class loaders, for example app servers, the context class loader\n     * will typically have been set to an appropriate loader for the current thread.\n     *\n     * <p>In the unusual case where the context class loader is null, the class loader that loaded\n     * this class ({@code Resources}) will be used instead.\n     *\n     * @throws IllegalArgumentException if the resource is not found\n     */\n    public static URL getResource(String resourceName) {\n        final ClassLoader contextClassLoader = Thread.currentThread().getContextClassLoader();\n        final ClassLoader loader = contextClassLoader == null ? Resources.class.getClassLoader() : contextClassLoader;\n        final URL url = loader.getResource(resourceName);\n        if (url == null) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"resource \" + resourceName + \" not found.\");\n        }\n        return url;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Reads all bytes from a URL into a byte array.\n     *\n     * @param url the URL to read from\n     * @return a byte array containing all the bytes from the URL\n     * @throws IOException if an I/O error occurs\n     */\n    public static byte[] toByteArray(URL url) throws IOException {\n        try (InputStream inputStream = url.openStream()) {\n            return ByteStreams.toByteArray(inputStream);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Reads all characters from a URL into a {@link String}, using the given character set.\n     *\n     * @param url     the URL to read from\n     * @param charset the charset used to decode the input stream; see {@link java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets} for helpful\n     *                predefined constants\n     * @return a string containing all the characters from the URL\n     * @throws IOException if an I/O error occurs.\n     */\n    public static String toString(URL url, Charset charset) throws IOException {\n        try (InputStream inputStream = url.openStream()) {\n            return new String(ByteStreams.toByteArray(inputStream), charset);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Copies all bytes from a URL to an output stream.\n     *\n     * @param from the URL to read from\n     * @param to the output stream\n     * @throws IOException if an I/O error occurs\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public static void copy(URL from, OutputStream to) throws IOException {\n        try (InputStream inputStream = from.openStream()) {\n            ByteStreams.copyInternal(inputStream, to);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.util;\n\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.HashSet;\nimport java.util.Set;\n\nimport static java.util.Collections.unmodifiableSet;\n\n/**\n * @since 2.0\n */\npublic final class Sets {\n    private Sets() {\n    }\n\n    public static <T> Set<T> of(T e1, T e2) {\n        final Set<T> set = new HashSet<>(2);\n        set.add(e1);\n        set.add(e2);\n        return unmodifiableSet(set);\n    }\n\n    public static <T> Set<T> of(T e1, T e2, T e3) {\n        final Set<T> set = new HashSet<>(3);\n        set.add(e1);\n        set.add(e2);\n        set.add(e3);\n        return unmodifiableSet(set);\n    }\n\n    public static <T> Set<T> of(T e1, T e2, T e3, T e4) {\n        final Set<T> set = new HashSet<>(4);\n        set.add(e1);\n        set.add(e2);\n        set.add(e3);\n        set.add(e4);\n        return unmodifiableSet(set);\n    }\n\n    public static <T> Set<T> of(T e1, T e2, T e3, T e4, T e5) {\n        final Set<T> set = new HashSet<>(5);\n        set.add(e1);\n        set.add(e2);\n        set.add(e3);\n        set.add(e4);\n        set.add(e5);\n        return unmodifiableSet(set);\n    }\n\n    @SafeVarargs\n    public static <T> Set<T> of(T... elements) {\n        final Set<T> set = new HashSet<>(elements.length);\n        set.addAll(Arrays.asList(elements));\n        return unmodifiableSet(set);\n    }\n\n    public static <T> Set<T> of(Iterable<T> elements) {\n        final Set<T> set = new HashSet<>();\n        for (T element : elements) {\n            set.add(element);\n        }\n        return unmodifiableSet(set);\n    }\n}\n"

java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.Locale;\nimport java.util.SortedMap;\nimport java.util.TreeMap;\nimport java.util.regex.Matcher;\nimport java.util.regex.Pattern;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\n/**\n * @deprecated Use {@link DataSize} for correct SI and IEC prefixes.\n */\n@Deprecated\npublic class Size implements Comparable<Size>, Serializable {\n    private static final long serialVersionUID = 6790991929249604526L;\n\n    private static final Pattern SIZE_PATTERN = Pattern.compile(\"(\\\\d+)\\\\s*(\\\\S+)\");\n    private static final SortedMap<String, SizeUnit> SUFFIXES;\n\n    static {\n        final SortedMap<String, SizeUnit> suffixes = new TreeMap<>(String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER);\n        suffixes.put(\"B\", SizeUnit.BYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"byte\", SizeUnit.BYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"bytes\", SizeUnit.BYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"K\", SizeUnit.KILOBYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"KB\", SizeUnit.KILOBYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"KiB\", SizeUnit.KILOBYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"kilobyte\", SizeUnit.KILOBYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"kilobytes\", SizeUnit.KILOBYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"M\", SizeUnit.MEGABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"MB\", SizeUnit.MEGABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"MiB\", SizeUnit.MEGABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"megabyte\", SizeUnit.MEGABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"megabytes\", SizeUnit.MEGABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"G\", SizeUnit.GIGABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"GB\", SizeUnit.GIGABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"GiB\", SizeUnit.GIGABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"gigabyte\", SizeUnit.GIGABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"gigabytes\", SizeUnit.GIGABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"T\", SizeUnit.TERABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"TB\", SizeUnit.TERABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"TiB\", SizeUnit.TERABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"terabyte\", SizeUnit.TERABYTES);\n        suffixes.put(\"terabytes\", SizeUnit.TERABYTES);\n        SUFFIXES = Collections.unmodifiableSortedMap(suffixes);\n    }\n\n    public static Size bytes(long count) {\n        return new Size(count, SizeUnit.BYTES);\n    }\n\n    public static Size kilobytes(long count) {\n        return new Size(count, SizeUnit.KILOBYTES);\n    }\n\n    public static Size megabytes(long count) {\n        return new Size(count, SizeUnit.MEGABYTES);\n    }\n\n    public static Size gigabytes(long count) {\n        return new Size(count, SizeUnit.GIGABYTES);\n    }\n\n    public static Size terabytes(long count) {\n        return new Size(count, SizeUnit.TERABYTES);\n    }\n\n    @JsonCreator\n    public static Size parse(String size) {\n        final Matcher matcher = SIZE_PATTERN.matcher(size);\n        if (!matcher.matches()) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Invalid size: \" + size);\n        }\n\n        final long count = Long.parseLong(matcher.group(1));\n        final SizeUnit unit = SUFFIXES.get(matcher.group(2));\n        if (unit == null) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Invalid size: \" + size + \". Wrong size unit\");\n        }\n\n        return new Size(count, unit);\n    }\n\n    private final long count;\n    private final SizeUnit unit;\n\n    private Size(long count, SizeUnit unit) {\n        this.count = count;\n        this.unit = requireNonNull(unit);\n    }\n\n    public long getQuantity() {\n        return count;\n    }\n\n    public SizeUnit getUnit() {\n        return unit;\n    }\n\n    public long toBytes() {\n        return SizeUnit.BYTES.convert(count, unit);\n    }\n\n    public long toKilobytes() {\n        return SizeUnit.KILOBYTES.convert(count, unit);\n    }\n\n    public long toMegabytes() {\n        return SizeUnit.MEGABYTES.convert(count, unit);\n    }\n\n    public long toGigabytes() {\n        return SizeUnit.GIGABYTES.convert(count, unit);\n    }\n\n    public long toTerabytes() {\n        return SizeUnit.TERABYTES.convert(count, unit);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean equals(Object obj) {\n        if (this == obj) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        if ((obj == null) || (getClass() != obj.getClass())) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        final Size size = (Size) obj;\n        return this.compareTo(size) == 0;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public int hashCode() {\n        return (31 * (int) (count ^ (count >>> 32))) + unit.hashCode();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    @JsonValue\n    public String toString() {\n        String units = unit.toString().toLowerCase(Locale.ENGLISH);\n        if (count == 1) {\n            units = units.substring(0, units.length() - 1);\n        }\n        return Long.toString(count) + ' ' + units;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public int compareTo(Size other) {\n        if (unit == other.unit) {\n            return Long.compare(count, other.count);\n        }\n\n        return Long.compare(toBytes(), other.toBytes());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    public DataSize toDataSize() {\n        switch (unit) {\n            case BYTES:\n                return DataSize.bytes(count);\n            case KILOBYTES:\n                return DataSize.kibibytes(count);\n            case MEGABYTES:\n                return DataSize.mebibytes(count);\n            case GIGABYTES:\n                return DataSize.gibibytes(count);\n            case TERABYTES:\n                return DataSize.tebibytes(count);\n            default:\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Unknown unit: \" + getUnit());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @since 2.0\n     */\n    public static Size fromDataSize(DataSize dataSize) {\n        switch (dataSize.getUnit()) {\n            case BYTES:\n                return Size.bytes(dataSize.getQuantity());\n            case KIBIBYTES:\n                return Size.kilobytes(dataSize.getQuantity());\n            case KILOBYTES:\n                return Size.bytes(dataSize.toBytes());\n            case MEBIBYTES:\n                return Size.megabytes(dataSize.getQuantity());\n            case MEGABYTES:\n                return Size.bytes(dataSize.toBytes());\n            case GIBIBYTES:\n                return Size.gigabytes(dataSize.getQuantity());\n            case GIGABYTES:\n                return Size.bytes(dataSize.toBytes());\n            case TEBIBYTES:\n                return Size.terabytes(dataSize.getQuantity());\n            case TERABYTES:\n                return Size.bytes(dataSize.toBytes());\n            case PEBIBYTES:\n                return Size.terabytes(dataSize.toTebibytes() * 1024L);\n            case PETABYTES:\n                return Size.bytes(dataSize.toBytes());\n            default:\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Unknown unit: \" + dataSize.getUnit());\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.util;\n\nimport javax.annotation.Nullable;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\n/**\n * @since 2.0\n *\n * @deprecated \n */\n@Deprecated\npublic final class Strings {\n    private Strings() {\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the given string if it is non-null; the empty string otherwise.\n     *\n     * @param string the string to test and possibly return\n     * @return {@code string} itself if it is non-null; {@code \"\"} if it is null\n     */\n    public static String nullToEmpty(@Nullable String string) {\n        return (string == null) ? \"\" : string;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the given string if it is nonempty; {@code null} otherwise.\n     *\n     * @param string the string to test and possibly return\n     * @return {@code string} itself if it is nonempty; {@code null} if it is empty or null\n     */\n    @Nullable\n    public static String emptyToNull(@Nullable String string) {\n        return isNullOrEmpty(string) ? null : string;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Returns {@code true} if the given string is null or is the empty string.\n     *\n     * <p>Consider normalizing your string references with {@link #nullToEmpty}. If you do, you can\n     * use {@link String#isEmpty()} instead of this method, and you won't need special null-safe forms\n     * of methods like {@link String#toUpperCase} either. Or, if you'd like to normalize \"in the other\n     * direction,\" converting empty strings to {@code null}, you can use {@link #emptyToNull}.\n     *\n     * @param string a string reference to check\n     * @return {@code true} if the string is null or is the empty string\n     */\n    public static boolean isNullOrEmpty(@Nullable String string) {\n        return string == null || string.isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Returns a string consisting of a specific number of concatenated copies of an input string. For\n     * example, {@code repeat(\"hey\", 3)} returns the string {@code \"heyheyhey\"}.\n     *\n     * @param string any non-null string\n     * @param count the number of times to repeat it; a nonnegative integer\n     * @return a string containing {@code string} repeated {@code count} times (the empty string if\n     *     {@code count} is zero)\n     * @throws IllegalArgumentException if {@code count} is negative\n     */\n    public static String repeat(String string, int count) {\n        requireNonNull(string);\n\n        if (count <= 1) {\n            if (count < 0) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"invalid count: \" + count);\n            }\n            return (count == 0) ? \"\" : string;\n        }\n\n        // IF YOU MODIFY THE CODE HERE, you must update StringsRepeatBenchmark\n        final int len = string.length();\n        final long longSize = (long) len * (long) count;\n        final int size = (int) longSize;\n        if (size != longSize) {\n            throw new ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException(\"Required array size too large: \" + longSize);\n        }\n\n        final char[] array = new char[size];\n        string.getChars(0, len, array, 0);\n        int n;\n        for (n = len; n < size - n; n <<= 1) {\n            System.arraycopy(array, 0, array, n, n);\n        }\n        System.arraycopy(array, 0, array, n, size - n);\n        return new String(array);\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-util/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/util/OptionalsTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.util;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nclass OptionalsTest {\n    @Test\n    void testFromGuavaOptional() throws Exception {\n        assertFalse(Optionals.fromGuavaOptional(com.google.common.base.Optional.absent()).isPresent());\n        assertTrue(Optionals.fromGuavaOptional(com.google.common.base.Optional.of(\"Foo\")).isPresent());\n        assertEquals(\n            java.util.Optional.of(\"Foo\"),\n            Optionals.fromGuavaOptional(com.google.common.base.Optional.of(\"Foo\"))\n        );\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void testToGuavaOptional() throws Exception {\n        assertFalse(Optionals.toGuavaOptional(java.util.Optional.empty()).isPresent());\n        assertTrue(Optionals.toGuavaOptional(java.util.Optional.of(\"Foo\")).isPresent());\n        assertEquals(\n            com.google.common.base.Optional.of(\"Foo\"),\n            Optionals.toGuavaOptional(java.util.Optional.of(\"Foo\"))\n        );\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "dropwizard-util/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/util/StringsTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.util;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatExceptionOfType;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatIllegalArgumentException;\n\nclass StringsTest {\n\n  @Test\n  void emptyToNull() {\n      assertThat(Strings.emptyToNull(\"A\"))\n          .isEqualTo(\"A\");\n      assertThat(Strings.emptyToNull(\"\")).isNull();\n      assertThat(Strings.emptyToNull(null)).isNull();\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void isNullOrEmpty() {\n      assertThat(Strings.isNullOrEmpty(\"A\")).isFalse();\n      assertThat(Strings.isNullOrEmpty(\"\")).isTrue();\n      assertThat(Strings.isNullOrEmpty(null)).isTrue();\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void nullToEmpty() {\n      assertThat(Strings.nullToEmpty(\"\")).isEmpty();\n      assertThat(Strings.nullToEmpty(null)).isEmpty();\n      assertThat(Strings.nullToEmpty(\"foo\")).isEqualTo(\"foo\");\n  }\n\n  @Test\n  void repeatExceptions() {\n      assertThatIllegalArgumentException().isThrownBy(() -> Strings.repeat(\"\", -2_147_483_647))\n        .withMessage(\"invalid count: -2147483647\" );\n      assertThatExceptionOfType(ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException.class).isThrownBy(() -> Strings.repeat(\"00000000\", 319_979_524))\n        .withMessage(\"Required array size too large: 2559836192\");\n  }\n\n\n  @Test\n  void repeat() {\n      assertThat(Strings.repeat(\"\", 0)).isEmpty();\n      assertThat(Strings.repeat(\"0\", 6)).isEqualTo(\"000000\");\n  }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-validation/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/validation/MaxSizeValidator.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.validation;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Size;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.SizeUnit;\n\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintValidator;\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintValidatorContext;\n\n/**\n * Check that a {@link Size} being validated is less than or equal to the\n * minimum value specified.\n */\npublic class MaxSizeValidator implements ConstraintValidator<MaxSize, Size> {\n\n    private long maxQty = 0;\n    private SizeUnit maxUnit = SizeUnit.BYTES;\n\n    @Override\n    public void initialize(MaxSize constraintAnnotation) {\n        this.maxQty = constraintAnnotation.value();\n        this.maxUnit = constraintAnnotation.unit();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isValid(Size value, ConstraintValidatorContext context) {\n        return (value == null) || (value.toBytes() <= maxUnit.toBytes(maxQty));\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.validation;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Size;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.SizeUnit;\n\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintValidator;\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintValidatorContext;\n\n/**\n * Check that a {@link Size} being validated is greater than or equal to the\n * minimum value specified.\n */\npublic class MinSizeValidator implements ConstraintValidator<MinSize, Size> {\n\n    private long minQty = 0;\n    private SizeUnit minUnit = SizeUnit.BYTES;\n\n    @Override\n    public void initialize(MinSize constraintAnnotation) {\n        this.minQty = constraintAnnotation.value();\n        this.minUnit = constraintAnnotation.unit();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isValid(Size value, ConstraintValidatorContext context) {\n        return (value == null) || (value.toBytes() >= minUnit.toBytes(minQty));\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "package io.dropwizard.validation;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.util.SizeUnit;\n\nimport javax.validation.Constraint;\nimport javax.validation.OverridesAttribute;\nimport javax.validation.Payload;\nimport javax.validation.ReportAsSingleViolation;\nimport java.lang.annotation.Documented;\nimport java.lang.annotation.Retention;\nimport java.lang.annotation.Target;\n\nimport static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.ANNOTATION_TYPE;\nimport static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.CONSTRUCTOR;\nimport static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.FIELD;\nimport static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.METHOD;\nimport static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.PARAMETER;\nimport static java.lang.annotation.ElementType.TYPE_USE;\nimport static java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME;\n\n/**\n * The annotated element has to be in the appropriate range. Apply on\n * {@link io.dropwizard.util.Size} instances.\n */\n@Documented\n@Constraint(validatedBy = { })\n@Target({ METHOD, FIELD, ANNOTATION_TYPE, CONSTRUCTOR, PARAMETER, TYPE_USE })\n@Retention(RUNTIME)\n@MinSize(0)\n@MaxSize(value = Long.MAX_VALUE, unit = SizeUnit.TERABYTES)\n@ReportAsSingleViolation\npublic @interface SizeRange {\n    @OverridesAttribute(constraint = MinSize.class, name = \"value\")\n    long min() default 0;\n\n    @OverridesAttribute(constraint = MaxSize.class, name = \"value\")\n    long max() default Long.MAX_VALUE;\n\n    @OverridesAttribute.List({\n        @OverridesAttribute(constraint = MinSize.class, name = \"unit\"),\n        @OverridesAttribute(constraint = MaxSize.class, name = \"unit\")\n    })\n    SizeUnit unit() default SizeUnit.BYTES;\n\n    String message() default \"must be between {min} {unit} and {max} {unit}\";\n\n    Class<?>[] groups() default { };\n\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"UnusedDeclaration\") Class<? extends Payload>[] payload() default { };\n\n    /**\n     * Defines several {@code @SizeRange} annotations on the same element.\n     */\n    @Target({ METHOD, FIELD, ANNOTATION_TYPE, CONSTRUCTOR, PARAMETER, TYPE_USE })\n    @Retention(RUNTIME)\n    @Documented\n    public @interface List {\n        SizeRange[] value();\n    }\n}\n"

      "path": "dropwizard-validation/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/validation/selfvalidating/SelfValidatingValidator.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.validation.selfvalidating;\n\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.AnnotationConfiguration;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.AnnotationInclusion;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.MemberResolver;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.ResolvedTypeWithMembers;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.TypeResolver;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.members.ResolvedMethod;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintValidator;\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintValidatorContext;\nimport java.lang.reflect.Method;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentMap;\nimport java.util.stream.Collectors;\n\n/**\n * This class is the base validator for the <code>@SelfValidating</code> annotation. It\n * initiates the self validation process on an object, generating wrapping methods to call\n * the validation methods efficiently and then calls them.\n */\npublic class SelfValidatingValidator implements ConstraintValidator<SelfValidating, Object> {\n    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(SelfValidatingValidator.class);\n\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"rawtypes\")\n    private final ConcurrentMap<Class<?>, List<ValidationCaller>> methodMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();\n    private final AnnotationConfiguration annotationConfiguration = new AnnotationConfiguration.StdConfiguration(AnnotationInclusion.INCLUDE_AND_INHERIT_IF_INHERITED);\n    private final TypeResolver typeResolver = new TypeResolver();\n    private final MemberResolver memberResolver = new MemberResolver(typeResolver);\n\n    @SuppressWarnings({\"unchecked\", \"rawtypes\"})\n    @Override\n    public boolean isValid(Object value, ConstraintValidatorContext context) {\n        final ViolationCollector collector = new ViolationCollector(context);\n        context.disableDefaultConstraintViolation();\n        for (ValidationCaller caller : methodMap.computeIfAbsent(value.getClass(), this::findMethods)) {\n            caller.setValidationObject(value);\n            caller.call(collector);\n        }\n        return !collector.hasViolationOccurred();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * This method generates <code>ValidationCaller</code>s for each method annotated\n     * with <code>@SelfValidation</code> that adheres to required signature.\n     */\n    @SuppressWarnings({ \"rawtypes\" })\n    private <T> List<ValidationCaller> findMethods(Class<T> annotated) {\n        ResolvedTypeWithMembers annotatedType = memberResolver.resolve(typeResolver.resolve(annotated), annotationConfiguration, null);\n        final List<ValidationCaller> callers = Arrays.stream(annotatedType.getMemberMethods())\n            .filter(this::isValidationMethod)\n            .filter(this::isMethodCorrect)\n            .map(m -> new ProxyValidationCaller<>(annotated, m))\n            .collect(Collectors.toList());\n        if (callers.isEmpty()) {\n            log.warn(\"The class {} is annotated with @SelfValidating but contains no valid methods that are annotated \" +\n                \"with @SelfValidation\", annotated);\n        }\n        return callers;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isValidationMethod(ResolvedMethod m) {\n        return m.get(SelfValidation.class) != null;\n    }\n\n    boolean isMethodCorrect(ResolvedMethod m) {\n        if (m.getReturnType()!=null) {\n            log.error(\"The method {} is annotated with @SelfValidation but does not return void. It is ignored\", m.getRawMember());\n            return false;\n        } else if (m.getArgumentCount() != 1 || !m.getArgumentType(0).getErasedType().equals(ViolationCollector.class)) {\n            log.error(\"The method {} is annotated with @SelfValidation but does not have a single parameter of type {}\",\n                m.getRawMember(), ViolationCollector.class);\n            return false;\n        } else if (!m.isPublic()) {\n            log.error(\"The method {} is annotated with @SelfValidation but is not public\", m.getRawMember());\n            return false;\n        }\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    static final class ProxyValidationCaller<T> extends ValidationCaller<T> {\n        private final Class<T> cls;\n        private final ResolvedMethod resolvedMethod;\n\n        ProxyValidationCaller(Class<T> cls, ResolvedMethod resolvedMethod) {\n            this.cls = cls;\n            this.resolvedMethod = resolvedMethod;\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public void call(ViolationCollector vc) {\n            final Method method = resolvedMethod.getRawMember();\n            final T obj = cls.cast(getValidationObject());\n            try {\n                method.invoke(obj, vc);\n            } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {\n                throw new IllegalStateException(\"Couldn't call \" + resolvedMethod + \" on \" + getValidationObject(), e);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-validation/src/main/java/io/dropwizard/validation/selfvalidating/ViolationCollector.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.validation.selfvalidating;\n\nimport org.hibernate.validator.constraintvalidation.HibernateConstraintValidatorContext;\n\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintValidatorContext;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.Map;\n\nimport static io.dropwizard.validation.InterpolationHelper.escapeMessageParameter;\n\n/**\n * This class is a simple wrapper around the ConstraintValidatorContext of hibernate validation.\n * It collects all the violations of the SelfValidation methods of an object.\n */\npublic class ViolationCollector {\n    private final ConstraintValidatorContext constraintValidatorContext;\n    private boolean violationOccurred = false;\n\n    public ViolationCollector(ConstraintValidatorContext constraintValidatorContext) {\n        this.constraintValidatorContext = constraintValidatorContext;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds a new violation to this collector. This also sets {@code violationOccurred} to {@code true}.\n     * <p>\n     * Prefer the method with explicit message parameters if you want to interpolate the message.\n     *\n     * @param message the message of the violation\n     * @see #addViolation(String, Map)\n     */\n    public void addViolation(String message) {\n        addViolation(message, Collections.emptyMap());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds a new violation to this collector. This also sets {@code violationOccurred} to {@code true}.\n     *\n     * @param message           the message of the violation\n     * @param messageParameters a map of message parameters which can be interpolated in the violation message\n     * @since 2.0.3\n     */\n    public void addViolation(String message, Map<String, Object> messageParameters) {\n        violationOccurred = true;\n        getContextWithMessageParameters(messageParameters)\n                .buildConstraintViolationWithTemplate(message)\n                .addConstraintViolation();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds a new violation to this collector. This also sets {@code violationOccurred} to {@code true}.\n     * <p>\n     * Prefer the method with explicit message parameters if you want to interpolate the message.\n     *\n     * @param propertyName the name of the property\n     * @param message      the message of the violation\n     * @see #addViolation(String, String, Map)\n     * @since 2.0.2\n     */\n    public void addViolation(String propertyName, String message) {\n        addViolation(propertyName, message, Collections.emptyMap());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds a new violation to this collector. This also sets {@code violationOccurred} to {@code true}.\n     *\n     * @param propertyName      the name of the property\n     * @param message           the message of the violation\n     * @param messageParameters a map of message parameters which can be interpolated in the violation message\n     * @since 2.0.3\n     */\n    public void addViolation(String propertyName, String message, Map<String, Object> messageParameters) {\n        violationOccurred = true;\n        getContextWithMessageParameters(messageParameters)\n                .buildConstraintViolationWithTemplate(message)\n                .addPropertyNode(propertyName)\n                .addConstraintViolation();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds a new violation to this collector. This also sets {@code violationOccurred} to {@code true}.\n     * Prefer the method with explicit message parameters if you want to interpolate the message.\n     *\n     * @param propertyName the name of the property with the violation\n     * @param index        the index of the element with the violation\n     * @param message      the message of the violation (any EL expression will be escaped and not parsed)\n     * @see ViolationCollector#addViolation(String, Integer, String, Map)\n     * @since 2.0.2\n     */\n    public void addViolation(String propertyName, Integer index, String message) {\n        addViolation(propertyName, index, message, Collections.emptyMap());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds a new violation to this collector. This also sets {@code violationOccurred} to {@code true}.\n     *\n     * @param propertyName      the name of the property with the violation\n     * @param index             the index of the element with the violation\n     * @param message           the message of the violation\n     * @param messageParameters a map of message parameters which can be interpolated in the violation message\n     * @since 2.0.3\n     */\n    public void addViolation(String propertyName, Integer index, String message, Map<String, Object> messageParameters) {\n        violationOccurred = true;\n        getContextWithMessageParameters(messageParameters)\n                .buildConstraintViolationWithTemplate(message)\n                .addPropertyNode(propertyName)\n                .addBeanNode().inIterable().atIndex(index)\n                .addConstraintViolation();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds a new violation to this collector. This also sets {@code violationOccurred} to {@code true}.\n     *\n     * @param propertyName the name of the property with the violation\n     * @param key          the key of the element with the violation\n     * @param message      the message of the violation\n     * @since 2.0.2\n     */\n    public void addViolation(String propertyName, String key, String message) {\n        addViolation(propertyName, key, message, Collections.emptyMap());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds a new violation to this collector. This also sets {@code violationOccurred} to {@code true}.\n     *\n     * @param propertyName      the name of the property with the violation\n     * @param key               the key of the element with the violation\n     * @param message           the message of the violation\n     * @param messageParameters a map of message parameters which can be interpolated in the violation message\n     * @since 2.0.3\n     */\n    public void addViolation(String propertyName, String key, String message, Map<String, Object> messageParameters) {\n        violationOccurred = true;\n        final HibernateConstraintValidatorContext context = getContextWithMessageParameters(messageParameters);\n        context.buildConstraintViolationWithTemplate(message)\n                .addPropertyNode(propertyName)\n                .addBeanNode().inIterable().atKey(key)\n                .addConstraintViolation();\n    }\n\n    private HibernateConstraintValidatorContext getContextWithMessageParameters(Map<String, Object> messageParameters) {\n        final HibernateConstraintValidatorContext context =\n                constraintValidatorContext.unwrap(HibernateConstraintValidatorContext.class);\n        for (Map.Entry<String, Object> messageParameter : messageParameters.entrySet()) {\n            final Object value = messageParameter.getValue();\n            final String escapedValue = value == null ? null : escapeMessageParameter(value.toString());\n            context.addMessageParameter(messageParameter.getKey(), escapedValue);\n        }\n        return context;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * This method returns the wrapped context for raw access to the validation framework. If you use\n     * the context to add violations make sure to call <code>setViolationOccurred(true)</code>.\n     *\n     * @return the wrapped Hibernate ConstraintValidatorContext\n     */\n    public ConstraintValidatorContext getContext() {\n        return constraintValidatorContext;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return if any violation was collected\n     */\n    public boolean hasViolationOccurred() {\n        return violationOccurred;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Manually sets if a violation occurred. This is automatically set if <code>addViolation</code> is called.\n     *\n     * @param violationOccurred if any violation was collected\n     */\n    public void setViolationOccurred(boolean violationOccurred) {\n        this.violationOccurred = violationOccurred;\n    }\n\n}\n"
    },
