      "path": "dropwizard-validation/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/validation/MethodValidatorTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.validation;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.validation.Valid;\nimport javax.validation.Validator;\nimport java.util.Collection;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\n\n@SuppressWarnings({\"FieldMayBeFinal\", \"MethodMayBeStatic\", \"UnusedDeclaration\"})\nclass MethodValidatorTest {\n    public static class SubExample {\n        @ValidationMethod(message = \"also needs something special\")\n        public boolean isOK() {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static class Example {\n        @Valid\n        private SubExample subExample = new SubExample();\n\n        @ValidationMethod(message = \"must have a false thing\")\n        public boolean isFalse() {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        @ValidationMethod(message = \"must have a true thing\")\n        public boolean isTrue() {\n            return true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private final Validator validator = BaseValidator.newValidator();\n\n    @Test\n    void complainsAboutMethodsWhichReturnFalse() throws Exception {\n        final Collection<String> errors =\n                ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new Example()));\n\n        assertThat(errors)\n                .containsOnly(\"must have a false thing\",\n                              \"also needs something special\");\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "dropwizard-validation/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/validation/OneOfValidatorTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.validation;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.validation.Valid;\nimport javax.validation.Validator;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Locale;\n\nimport static io.dropwizard.validation.ConstraintViolations.format;\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assumptions.assumeTrue;\n\nclass OneOfValidatorTest {\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"UnusedDeclaration\")\n    public static class Example {\n        @OneOf({\"one\", \"two\", \"three\"})\n        private String basic = \"one\";\n\n        @OneOf(value = {\"one\", \"two\", \"three\"}, ignoreCase = true)\n        private String caseInsensitive = \"one\";\n\n        @OneOf(value = {\"one\", \"two\", \"three\"}, ignoreWhitespace = true)\n        private String whitespaceInsensitive = \"one\";\n\n        @Valid\n        private List<@OneOf({\"one\", \"two\", \"three\"}) String> basicList = Collections.singletonList(\"one\");\n    }\n\n    private final Validator validator = BaseValidator.newValidator();\n\n    @Test\n    void allowsExactElements() throws Exception {\n        assertThat(format(validator.validate(new Example())))\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void doesNotAllowOtherElements() throws Exception {\n        assumeTrue(\"en\".equals(Locale.getDefault().getLanguage()),\n                \"This test executes when the defined language is English ('en'). If not, it is skipped.\");\n\n        final Example example = new Example();\n        example.basic = \"four\";\n\n        assertThat(format(validator.validate(example)))\n                .containsOnly(\"basic must be one of [one, two, three]\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void doesNotAllowBadElementsInList() {\n        final Example example = new Example();\n        example.basicList = Collections.singletonList(\"four\");\n\n        assertThat(format(validator.validate(example)))\n            .containsOnly(\"basicList[0].<list element> must be one of [one, two, three]\");\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void optionallyIgnoresCase() throws Exception {\n        final Example example = new Example();\n        example.caseInsensitive = \"ONE\";\n\n        assertThat(format(validator.validate(example)))\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void optionallyIgnoresWhitespace() throws Exception {\n        final Example example = new Example();\n        example.whitespaceInsensitive = \"   one  \";\n\n        assertThat(format(validator.validate(example)))\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "dropwizard-validation/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/validation/SelfValidationTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.validation;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Maps;\nimport io.dropwizard.validation.selfvalidating.SelfValidating;\nimport io.dropwizard.validation.selfvalidating.SelfValidation;\nimport io.dropwizard.validation.selfvalidating.ViolationCollector;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\nimport uk.org.lidalia.slf4jext.Level;\nimport uk.org.lidalia.slf4jtest.LoggingEvent;\nimport uk.org.lidalia.slf4jtest.TestLoggerFactory;\n\nimport javax.annotation.concurrent.NotThreadSafe;\nimport javax.validation.Validator;\nimport java.util.Collections;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\n\n@NotThreadSafe\nclass SelfValidationTest {\n\n    private static final String FAILED = \"failed\";\n    private static final String FAILED_RESULT = \" \" + FAILED;\n\n    @AfterEach\n    @BeforeEach\n    public void clearAllLoggers() {\n        //this must be a clear all because the validation runs in other threads\n        TestLoggerFactory.clearAll();\n    }\n\n    @SelfValidating\n    public static class FailingExample {\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateFail(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.addViolation(FAILED);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static class SubclassExample extends FailingExample {\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void subValidateFail(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.addViolation(FAILED + \"subclass\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    @SelfValidating\n    public static class AnnotatedSubclassExample extends FailingExample {\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void subValidateFail(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.addViolation(FAILED + \"subclass\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static class OverridingExample extends FailingExample {\n        @Override\n        public void validateFail(ViolationCollector col) {\n        }\n    }\n\n    @SelfValidating\n    public static class DirectContextExample {\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateFail(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.getContext().buildConstraintViolationWithTemplate(FAILED).addConstraintViolation();\n            col.setViolationOccurred(true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @SelfValidating\n    public static class CorrectExample {\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateCorrect(ViolationCollector col) {\n        }\n    }\n\n    @SelfValidating\n    public static class InvalidExample {\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateCorrect(ViolationCollector col) {\n        }\n\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateFailAdditionalParameters(ViolationCollector col, int a) {\n            col.addViolation(FAILED);\n        }\n\n        @SelfValidation\n        public boolean validateFailReturn(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.addViolation(FAILED);\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        @SelfValidation\n        private void validateFailPrivate(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.addViolation(FAILED);\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    @SelfValidating\n    public static class ComplexExample {\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateFail1(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.addViolation(FAILED + \"1\");\n        }\n\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateFail2(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.addViolation(\"p2\", FAILED);\n        }\n\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateFail3(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.addViolation(\"p\", 3, FAILED);\n        }\n\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateFail4(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.addViolation(\"p\", \"four\", FAILED);\n        }\n\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateCorrect(ViolationCollector col) {\n        }\n    }\n\n    @SelfValidating\n    public static class NoValidations {\n    }\n\n    @SelfValidating\n    public static class InjectionExample {\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateFail(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.addViolation(\"${'value'}\");\n            col.addViolation(\"$\\\\A{1+1}\");\n            col.addViolation(\"{value}\", Collections.singletonMap(\"value\", \"TEST\"));\n            col.addViolation(\"${'property'}\", \"${'value'}\");\n            col.addViolation(\"${'property'}\", 1, \"${'value'}\");\n            col.addViolation(\"${'property'}\", \"${'key'}\", \"${'value'}\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    @SelfValidating\n    public static class MessageParametersExample {\n        @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateFail(ViolationCollector col) {\n            col.addViolation(\"{1+1}\");\n            col.addViolation(\"{value}\", Collections.singletonMap(\"value\", \"VALUE\"));\n            col.addViolation(\"No parameter\", Collections.singletonMap(\"value\", \"VALUE\"));\n            col.addViolation(\"{value} {unsetParameter}\", Collections.singletonMap(\"value\", \"VALUE\"));\n            col.addViolation(\"{value\", Collections.singletonMap(\"value\", \"VALUE\"));\n            col.addViolation(\"value}\", Collections.singletonMap(\"value\", \"VALUE\"));\n            col.addViolation(\"{  value  }\", Collections.singletonMap(\"value\", \"VALUE\"));\n            col.addViolation(\"Mixed ${'value'} {value}\", Collections.singletonMap(\"value\", \"VALUE\"));\n            col.addViolation(\"Nested {value}\", Collections.singletonMap(\"value\", \"${'nested'}\"));\n            col.addViolation(\"{property}\", \"{value}\", Maps.of(\"property\", \"PROPERTY\", \"value\", \"VALUE\"));\n            col.addViolation(\"{property}\", 1, \"{value}\", Maps.of(\"property\", \"PROPERTY\", \"value\", \"VALUE\"));\n            col.addViolation(\"{property}\", \"{key}\", \"{value}\", Maps.of(\"property\", \"PROPERTY\", \"key\", \"KEY\", \"value\", \"VALUE\"));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private final Validator validator = BaseValidator.newValidator();\n\n    @Test\n    void failingExample() {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new FailingExample())))\n                .containsExactlyInAnyOrder(FAILED_RESULT);\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents())\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void subClassExample() {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new SubclassExample())))\n                .containsExactlyInAnyOrder(\n                        FAILED_RESULT,\n                        FAILED_RESULT + \"subclass\"\n                );\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents())\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void annotatedSubClassExample() {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new AnnotatedSubclassExample())))\n                .containsExactlyInAnyOrder(\n                        FAILED_RESULT,\n                        FAILED_RESULT + \"subclass\"\n                );\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents())\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void overridingSubClassExample() {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new OverridingExample())))\n                .isEmpty();\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents())\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void correctExample() {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new CorrectExample())))\n                .isEmpty();\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents())\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void multipleTestingOfSameClass() {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new CorrectExample())))\n                .isEmpty();\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new CorrectExample())))\n                .isEmpty();\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents())\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void testDirectContextUsage() {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new DirectContextExample())))\n                .containsExactlyInAnyOrder(FAILED_RESULT);\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents())\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void complexExample() {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new ComplexExample())))\n                .containsExactly(\n                        \" failed1\",\n                        \"p2 failed\",\n                        \"p[3] failed\",\n                        \"p[four] failed\");\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents())\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void invalidExample() throws Exception {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new InvalidExample())))\n                .isEmpty();\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents())\n                .containsExactlyInAnyOrder(\n                        new LoggingEvent(\n                                Level.ERROR,\n                                \"The method {} is annotated with @SelfValidation but does not have a single parameter of type {}\",\n                                InvalidExample.class.getMethod(\"validateFailAdditionalParameters\", ViolationCollector.class, int.class),\n                                ViolationCollector.class\n                        ),\n                        new LoggingEvent(\n                                Level.ERROR,\n                                \"The method {} is annotated with @SelfValidation but does not return void. It is ignored\",\n                                InvalidExample.class.getMethod(\"validateFailReturn\", ViolationCollector.class)\n                        ),\n                        new LoggingEvent(\n                                Level.ERROR,\n                                \"The method {} is annotated with @SelfValidation but is not public\",\n                                InvalidExample.class.getDeclaredMethod(\"validateFailPrivate\", ViolationCollector.class)\n                        )\n                );\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void giveWarningIfNoValidationMethods() {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new NoValidations())))\n                .isEmpty();\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents())\n                .containsExactlyInAnyOrder(\n                        new LoggingEvent(\n                                Level.WARN,\n                                \"The class {} is annotated with @SelfValidating but contains no valid methods that are annotated with @SelfValidation\",\n                                NoValidations.class\n                        )\n\n                );\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void violationMessagesAreEscapedByDefault() {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new InjectionExample()))).containsExactly(\n                \" $\\\\A{1+1}\",\n                \" ${'value'}\",\n                \" TEST\",\n                \"${'property'} ${'value'}\",\n                \"${'property'}[${'key'}] ${'value'}\",\n                \"${'property'}[1] ${'value'}\"\n        );\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents()).isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void messageParametersExample() {\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new MessageParametersExample()))).containsExactly(\n                \" Mixed ${'value'} VALUE\",\n                \" Nested ${'nested'}\",\n                \" No parameter\",\n                \" VALUE\",\n                \" VALUE {unsetParameter}\",\n                \" value}\",\n                \" {  value  }\",\n                \" {1+1}\",\n                \" {value\",\n                \"{property} VALUE\",\n                \"{property}[1] VALUE\",\n                \"{property}[{key}] VALUE\"\n        );\n        assertThat(TestLoggerFactory.getAllLoggingEvents()).isEmpty();\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-validation/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/validation/SizeValidatorTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.validation;\n\nimport io.dropwizard.util.Size;\nimport io.dropwizard.util.SizeUnit;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.validation.Valid;\nimport javax.validation.Validator;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Locale;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assumptions.assumeTrue;\n\nclass SizeValidatorTest {\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"unused\")\n    public static class Example {\n        @MaxSize(value = 30, unit = SizeUnit.KILOBYTES)\n        private Size tooBig = Size.gigabytes(2);\n\n        @MinSize(value = 30, unit = SizeUnit.KILOBYTES)\n        private Size tooSmall = Size.bytes(100);\n\n        @SizeRange(min = 10, max = 100, unit = SizeUnit.KILOBYTES)\n        private Size outOfRange = Size.megabytes(2);\n\n        @Valid\n        private List<@MaxSize(value = 30, unit = SizeUnit.KILOBYTES) Size> maxSize =\n                Collections.singletonList(Size.gigabytes(2));\n\n        @Valid\n        private List<@MinSize(value = 30, unit = SizeUnit.KILOBYTES) Size> minSize =\n                Collections.singletonList(Size.bytes(100));\n\n        @Valid\n        private List<@SizeRange(min = 10, max = 100, unit = SizeUnit.KILOBYTES) Size> rangeSize =\n                Collections.singletonList(Size.megabytes(2));\n\n        public void setTooBig(Size tooBig) {\n            this.tooBig = tooBig;\n        }\n        public void setTooSmall(Size tooSmall) {\n            this.tooSmall = tooSmall;\n        }\n        public void setOutOfRange(Size outOfRange) {\n            this.outOfRange = outOfRange;\n        }\n        public void setMaxSize(List<Size> maxSize) {\n            this.maxSize = maxSize;\n        }\n        public void setMinSize(List<Size> minSize) {\n            this.minSize = minSize;\n        }\n        public void setRangeSize(List<Size> rangeSize) {\n            this.rangeSize = rangeSize;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private final Validator validator = BaseValidator.newValidator();\n\n    @Test\n    void returnsASetOfErrorsForAnObject() throws Exception {\n        assumeTrue(\"en\".equals(Locale.getDefault().getLanguage()),\n                \"This test executes when the defined language is English ('en'). If not, it is skipped.\");\n\n        assertThat(ConstraintViolations.format(validator.validate(new Example())))\n                    .containsOnly(\"outOfRange must be between 10 KILOBYTES and 100 KILOBYTES\",\n                                  \"tooBig must be less than or equal to 30 KILOBYTES\",\n                                  \"tooSmall must be greater than or equal to 30 KILOBYTES\",\n                                   \"maxSize[0].<list element> must be less than or equal to 30 KILOBYTES\",\n                                   \"minSize[0].<list element> must be greater than or equal to 30 KILOBYTES\",\n                                   \"rangeSize[0].<list element> must be between 10 KILOBYTES and 100 KILOBYTES\");\n\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void returnsAnEmptySetForAValidObject() throws Exception {\n        final Example example = new Example();\n        example.setTooBig(Size.bytes(10));\n        example.setTooSmall(Size.megabytes(10));\n        example.setOutOfRange(Size.kilobytes(64));\n        example.setMaxSize(Collections.singletonList(Size.bytes(10)));\n        example.setMinSize(Collections.singletonList(Size.megabytes(10)));\n        example.setRangeSize(Collections.singletonList(Size.kilobytes(64)));\n\n        assertThat(validator.validate(example))\n                .isEmpty();\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "dropwizard-validation/src/test/java/io/dropwizard/validation/selfvalidating/SelfValidatingValidatorTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package io.dropwizard.validation.selfvalidating;\n\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.AnnotationConfiguration;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.AnnotationInclusion;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.MemberResolver;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.ResolvedTypeWithMembers;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.TypeResolver;\nimport com.fasterxml.classmate.members.ResolvedMethod;\nimport io.dropwizard.validation.BaseValidator;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport javax.validation.ConstraintViolation;\nimport javax.validation.Validator;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.Set;\n\nimport static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;\n\nclass SelfValidatingValidatorTest {\n    private SelfValidatingValidator selfValidatingValidator = new SelfValidatingValidator();\n\n    @Test\n    void validObjectHasNoViolations() throws Exception {\n        final Validator validator = BaseValidator.newValidator();\n        final Set<ConstraintViolation<ValidExample>> violations = validator.validate(new ValidExample(1));\n        assertThat(violations).isEmpty();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void invalidObjectHasViolations() throws Exception {\n        final Validator validator = BaseValidator.newValidator();\n        final Set<ConstraintViolation<ValidExample>> violations = validator.validate(new ValidExample(-1));\n        assertThat(violations)\n                .isNotEmpty()\n                .allSatisfy(violation -> assertThat(violation.getMessage()).isEqualTo(\"n must be positive!\"));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void correctMethod() throws Exception {\n        assertThat(selfValidatingValidator.isMethodCorrect(\n                getMethod(\"validateCorrect\", ViolationCollector.class)))\n                .isTrue();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void voidIsNotAccepted() throws Exception {\n        assertThat(selfValidatingValidator.isMethodCorrect(\n                getMethod(\"validateFailReturn\", ViolationCollector.class)))\n                .isFalse();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void privateIsNotAccepted() throws Exception {\n        assertThat(selfValidatingValidator.isMethodCorrect(\n                getMethod(\"validateFailPrivate\", ViolationCollector.class)))\n                .isFalse();\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void additionalParametersAreNotAccepted() throws Exception {\n        assertThat(selfValidatingValidator.isMethodCorrect(\n                getMethod(\"validateFailAdditionalParameters\", ViolationCollector.class, int.class)))\n                .isFalse();\n    }\n\n    private ResolvedMethod getMethod(String name, Class<?>... params) {\n        AnnotationConfiguration annotationConfiguration = new AnnotationConfiguration.StdConfiguration(AnnotationInclusion.INCLUDE_AND_INHERIT_IF_INHERITED);\n        TypeResolver typeResolver = new TypeResolver();\n        MemberResolver memberResolver = new MemberResolver(typeResolver);\n        ResolvedTypeWithMembers annotatedType = memberResolver.resolve(typeResolver.resolve(InvalidExample.class), annotationConfiguration, null);\n        for (ResolvedMethod m : annotatedType.getMemberMethods()) {\n            if (hasSignature(m, name, params)) {\n                return m;\n            }\n        }\n        throw new IllegalStateException(\"Could not resolve method \" + name + Arrays.toString(params) + \" in \" + InvalidExample.class);\n    }\n\n    private boolean hasSignature(ResolvedMethod m, String name, Class<?>[] params) {\n        if (!m.getName().equals(name) || m.getArgumentCount() != params.length) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < params.length; i++) {\n            if (!m.getArgumentType(i).getErasedType().equals(params[i]))\n                return false;\n        }\n        return true;\n    }\n\n\n    @SelfValidating\n    static class InvalidExample {\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateCorrect(ViolationCollector col) {\n        }\n\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validateFailAdditionalParameters(ViolationCollector col, int a) {\n        }\n\n        @SelfValidation\n        public boolean validateFailReturn(ViolationCollector col) {\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        @SelfValidation\n        private void validateFailPrivate(ViolationCollector col) {\n        }\n    }\n\n    @SelfValidating\n    static class ValidExample {\n        final int n;\n\n        ValidExample(int n) {\n            this.n = n;\n        }\n\n        @SelfValidation\n        public void validate(ViolationCollector col) {\n            if (n < 0) {\n                col.addViolation(\"n must be positive!\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },
